#### Skills and commands: notes

- **Description length, 1,024 vs 1,536.** The Agent Skills spec caps `description` at 1,024 characters and upload fails above it. Claude Code truncates `description` plus `when_to_use` at 1,536 characters in the listing. `skill-description-max-length` checks each limit against its own text: 1,024 on `description` alone, and 1,536 on `description` plus `when_to_use`. One limit on the combined text would be stricter than both sources. The 1,536 cap is configurable with `skillListingMaxDescChars`. The changelog also records an older 250-character cap, raised to 1,536. [^skills-frontmatter-reference] [^platform-agent-skills-best-practices-yaml-frontmatter-requirements] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog]
- **500 lines: file or body.** skills.md says "Keep `SKILL.md` under 500 lines"; best-practices says "SKILL.md body". The default counts the whole file (stricter). [^skills-add-supporting-files] [^platform-agent-skills-best-practices-token-budgets]
- **Listing budget.** skills.md and env-vars.md give 1% of the context window, with an 8,000-character fallback. An older changelog entry says 2%. The rule uses the later value. The budget depends on the model, so `skill-listing-budget` is a heuristic. [^skills-skill-descriptions-are-cut-short] [^env-vars-variables]
- **`name` required.** The platform pages require `name`; Claude Code makes every field optional and falls back to the directory name. Only `skill-spec-frontmatter-only` requires it. [^platform-agent-skills-overview-skill-structure] [^skills-frontmatter-reference]
- **`skill-spec-frontmatter-only` is `off` although exact.** It fails only on upload outside Claude Code, and in `recommended` it would flag every Claude Code-only field (`disable-model-invocation`, `argument-hint`, ...). This diverges from the policy table (which gives recommended warn); enable it per path for skills you upload. [^skills-using-skill-frontmatter-outside-claude-code]
- **Command frontmatter.** skills.md says `.claude/commands/` files accept the skill fields except `name` and `paths`. plugins/components says plugin command files "take the same frontmatter as skills". `skill-frontmatter-schema` applies the exception to both. [^skills-frontmatter-reference] [^plugins-components-commands]
- **`AskUserQuestion` in `allowed-tools`.** The finding was best-practice. Since the changelog fix, listing it no longer auto-allows it, so the entry has no effect. It is folded into the `no-op` rule `skill-allowed-tools-ineffective`. [^changelog-claude-code-changelog]
- **`skill-paths-glob-valid`** relies on "Uses the same format as path-specific rules" plus the changelog fix that bounds `SKILL.md` brace expansion. It mirrors the memory group's rules glob rule. [^skills-frontmatter-reference] [^memory-path-specific-rules]
- **Validate coverage.** A changelog entry says validate checks skill and command frontmatter for "YAML parse errors and schema violations", but no page lists the schema checks. Unknown keys are ignored at load. Only parse failure and a missing frontmatter block are marked `covered`. validate does not run on a repo's `.claude/commands/`. [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] [^skills-skill-not-triggering]
- **Footnote headings.** "Choose where skills load" and "Names reserved for synced skills" are HTML headings with ids that differ from their slugs (`where-skills-live`, `names-reserved-for-synced-skills`). Footnotes use the nearest Markdown heading above them. The changelog has one heading; each Checks cell names the version instead.
- **Owned by other groups (not repeated here):** `skillOverrides`, `skillListing*`, `disableSkillShellExecution` and the `SLASH_COMMAND_TOOL_CHAR_BUDGET` env name (settings); `Skill(...)` rule syntax (permissions); hooks in skill frontmatter and `once` (hooks); an agent's `skills:` list naming a `disable-model-invocation` skill (agents); plugin-root `CLAUDE.md`, the `skills` and `commands` manifest keys, symlinked component entries, and `${user_config.KEY}` of a sensitive option in skill content (plugin); an MCP server named `anthropic-skills` (mcp-lsp); CLAUDE.md length (memory).
- **bje candidates (not rules yet).** This repo's CLAUDE.md requires `docs/flows/<plugin>/<skill>/_skill-flow.md` for every skill, and `_design-principles.md` forbids `disable-model-invocation: true` on a skill that another skill invokes and says new plugins use `skills/`. The Proposed `bje` preset section raises `command-legacy-format` to `error`. The flow-docs check stays in `tests/repo/` (ADR 005 Decision 2).

### Subagents and output styles

Covers subagent files (`.claude/agents/**/*.md`, plugin `agents/**/*.md`), output style files (`.claude/output-styles/*.md`, plugin `output-styles/*.md`), and agent teams configuration: 46 rules. "Local" means a project, user, managed or `--add-dir` agent file; "plugin" means a file under a plugin's `agents/` directory or its `agents` manifest key.

| Rule | Checks | Preset | Severity | Category | Validate | Docs |
|------|--------|--------|----------|----------|----------|------|
| `agent-frontmatter-valid` | Local agents only. Line 1 is `---`; YAML parses; `name` is present; `description` is present; `name` does not start with `-` and has no `:`. On any failure Claude Code skips the file silently (treated as documentation or logged to the debug log only). | recommended | error | load | covered (YAML parse only, v2.1.233+, when pointed at `.claude/agents`; not a missing `name`) | [^sub-agents-subagent-files-claude-code-skips] [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] [^sub-agents-check-an-agents-directory-before-a-session] [^glossary-frontmatter] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `agent-plugin-frontmatter-valid` | Plugin agents only. A frontmatter block exists and parses. A plugin agent that fails still loads, with every field ignored, named after the file, description `Agent from <plugin> plugin`. A missing `name` alone is valid (file name is used). | recommended | error | no-op | covered (`No frontmatter block found` warns, fails under `--strict`; parse failure is an error) | [^plugins-components-frontmatter-fields-in-plugin-agents] [^sub-agents-subagent-files-claude-code-skips] [^plugins-troubleshooting-claude-plugin-validate-reports-errors] |
| `agent-no-bom` | Agent `.md` file does not start with a UTF-8 BOM. Before v2.1.239 such a file was silently ignored. Condition: older Claude Code versions. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `agent-frontmatter-schema` | Keys in {`name`, `description`, `tools`, `disallowedTools`, `model`, `permissionMode`, `maxTurns`, `skills`, `mcpServers`, `hooks`, `memory`, `background`, `omitClaudeMd`, `effort`, `isolation`, `color`, `initialPrompt`, `experimental`}, exact camelCase (flag `max_turns`, `disallowed-tools`). Types and enums: see Notes. Unknown keys are ignored without error. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] [^claude-directory-frontmatter-fields-by-file] [^agent-sdk-subagents-agentdefinition-configuration] [^plugins-components-frontmatter-fields-in-plugin-agents] |
| `agent-plugin-ignored-fields` | Plugin agents do not set `permissionMode`, `hooks`, `mcpServers` or `initialPrompt`; Claude Code ignores them for plugin agents. Move hooks and MCP servers to plugin `hooks/hooks.json` and `.mcp.json`. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-components-frontmatter-fields-in-plugin-agents] [^sub-agents-choose-the-subagent-scope] |
| `agent-field-min-version` | Flags fields that need a minimum Claude Code version, against option `minVersion`: `omitClaudeMd` v2.1.271, `experimental.cacheTtl` v2.1.248, `permissionMode: manual` v2.1.200. Condition: older versions. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] |
| `agent-name-unique` | Two local files under the same `.claude/agents/` tree (subfolders included) do not declare the same `name`. Only one loads, by filesystem read order. Subfolders do not change identity. (cross-file) | recommended | error | consistency | – | [^sub-agents-choose-the-subagent-scope] |
| `agent-name-shadowing` | Same `name` in nested project `.claude/agents/` directories between a subdirectory and the repo root: the one closest to the working directory wins. (cross-file) | recommended | warn | consistency | – | [^sub-agents-choose-the-subagent-scope] |
| `agent-name-shadows-builtin` | Local agent `name` equals a built-in (`Explore`, `Plan`, `general-purpose`, `claude`, `statusline-setup`, `claude-code-guide`) and overrides it. Option `allow` for intended overrides. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^sub-agents-built-in-subagents] |
| `agent-plugin-scoped-name-unique` | Plugin scoped ids `<plugin>:<subfolders>:<name or file name>` are unique in the plugin. Files listed in manifest `agents` drop subfolder segments, so they can collide. Subfolder names contain no `:`. (cross-file) | recommended | warn | consistency | – | [^plugins-components-organize-agents-in-subfolders] [^plugins-components-agents] |
| `agent-tools-known` | Each `tools`/`disallowedTools` entry is a canonical built-in tool name (mcp-lsp appendix), `Agent`/`Agent(a, b)`, or `mcp__<server>`, `mcp__<server>__*`, `mcp__<server>__<tool>`. `mcp__*` only in `disallowedTools`. Value is a comma string or YAML list. | recommended | error | load | – | [^sub-agents-available-tools] [^sub-agents-restrict-which-subagents-can-be-spawned] [^errors-agent-would-be-spawned-with-zero-tools] |
| `agent-tools-unavailable` | `tools` lists a tool always removed from subagents: `AskUserQuestion`, `EndConversation`, `EnterPlanMode`, `ScheduleWakeup`, `WaitForMcpServers`, `Workflow`; `ExitPlanMode` without `permissionMode: plan`. With `background: true`, any built-in outside the background set. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^sub-agents-available-tools] [^errors-agent-would-be-spawned-with-zero-tools] |
| `agent-tools-conditional` | `background` unset and `tools` lists a built-in outside the background set: removed in background runs, the default. `tools` is only `Agent`: resolves to nothing at the depth limit. Condition: run mode and depth. | recommended | warn | no-op | – | [^sub-agents-available-tools] [^errors-agent-would-be-spawned-with-zero-tools] |
| `agent-disallowed-tools-scope` | A `disallowedTools` entry with a specifier (`Bash(git push *)`) removes the whole tool; use `permissions.deny`. A tool in both `tools` and `disallowedTools` is removed. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^sub-agents-available-tools] |
| `agent-tools-task-alias` | `Task` or `Task(...)` in `tools`/`disallowedTools`: renamed to `Agent` in v2.1.63; still works as an alias. | recommended | warn | deprecated | – | [^sub-agents-restrict-which-subagents-can-be-spawned] |
| `agent-tools-agent-type-list` | `Agent(a, b)` type list is used only when the agent runs as main thread (`--agent`, `agent` setting); it is ignored in a subagent. Listed types name known agents. (heuristic, cross-file) | off | warn | no-op | – | [^sub-agents-restrict-which-subagents-can-be-spawned] |
| `agent-tools-skill-for-preload` | `Skill` in `tools` with no `skills` field: to preload skills use `skills`. (heuristic) | off | warn | practice | – | [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] |
| `agent-skills-preloadable` | Each `skills` entry that resolves to a repo skill does not set `disable-model-invocation: true` (bundled `verify` included). Such skills are never preloaded. (cross-file) | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^sub-agents-preload-skills-into-subagents] [^skills-frontmatter-reference] |
| `agent-skills-exist` | Each `skills` entry resolves to a project or plugin skill; missing ones are skipped with a debug-log warning. Also reports total preloaded `SKILL.md` size (full content is injected). (heuristic: user and managed skills are not visible) | off | warn | consistency | – | [^sub-agents-preload-skills-into-subagents] [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] |
| `agent-mcp-servers-schema` | `mcpServers` is a list; each entry is a string or a single-key map `name: config`. Inline config follows the `.mcp.json` server schema (mcp-lsp rules) with `type` in `stdio`, `http`, `sse`, `ws`; `sdk` is skipped with a warning. | recommended | error | load | – | [^sub-agents-scope-mcp-servers-to-a-subagent] [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `agent-mcp-servers-inline-trust` | Inline server in a project `.claude/agents/` file: it connects and runs its command only after that folder is trusted (parent trust and `-p` do not count). Review the command. | recommended | warn | security | – | [^sub-agents-scope-mcp-servers-to-a-subagent] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `agent-mcp-servers-ref-exists` | Each string `mcpServers` entry names a server in `.mcp.json` or committed settings. (heuristic: user config is not visible) | off | warn | consistency | – | [^sub-agents-scope-mcp-servers-to-a-subagent] |
| `agent-permission-mode-bypass` | Local agent sets `permissionMode: bypassPermissions`. Since v2.1.267 it is ignored unless the main session already bypasses; earlier versions granted bypass. | recommended | error | security | – | [^sub-agents-permission-modes] |
| `agent-permission-mode-manual` | `permissionMode: manual` is an alias; write the config value `default`. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^sub-agents-permission-modes] [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] |
| `agent-memory-auto-memory-off` | Agent sets `memory` while committed settings set `autoMemoryEnabled: false` or `env.CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY`: `memory` has no effect. (cross-file) | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^sub-agents-enable-persistent-memory] |
| `agent-memory-grants-write` | Agent sets `memory` and a `tools` list without `Write` or `Edit`: memory enables Read, Write and Edit anyway. | recommended | error | security | – | [^sub-agents-enable-persistent-memory] |
| `agent-memory-scope-project` | `memory` is `user` or `local`; `project` is the recommended default scope. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^sub-agents-persistent-memory-tips] |
| `agent-omit-claude-md-main` | Agent named by committed settings `agent` sets `omitClaudeMd`: ignored for the main session agent. (cross-file) | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] |
| `agent-initial-prompt-main-only` | `initialPrompt` applies only when the agent runs as main session (`--agent`, `agent` setting). Flag when no committed `agent` setting names the agent. (heuristic, cross-file) | off | warn | no-op | – | [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] |
| `agent-model-forced` | Agent sets `model` while committed settings set `env.CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL_FORCE` on: every agent `model` is ignored. (cross-file) | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^sub-agents-run-every-subagent-on-one-model] |
| `agent-model-value` | `model` is `sonnet`, `opus`, `haiku`, `fable`, `inherit`, or a `claude-` model ID. (heuristic: provider IDs such as Bedrock ARNs are also valid) | off | warn | load | – | [^sub-agents-choose-a-model] [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] |
| `agent-descriptions-budget` | Combined `name` + `description` of all non-built-in agents is at most option `maxTokens` 15,000 (estimated with option `charsPerToken`); over it, a startup warning. (heuristic: no documented char proxy) | off | warn | limit | – | [^sub-agents-create-custom-subagents] [^errors-agent-descriptions-are-over-the-150k-token-limit] |
| `agent-description-proactive` | `description` contains "use proactively" or similar, to encourage delegation. (heuristic) | off | warn | practice | – | [^sub-agents-understand-automatic-delegation] |
| `agent-body-nonempty` | Body after the frontmatter is not empty; it is the agent's whole system prompt. (heuristic) | off | warn | practice | – | [^sub-agents-write-subagent-files] |
| `agent-name-kebab-case` | `name` matches `^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$`, as the documented examples do. Never require `name` to equal the file name. (heuristic) | off | warn | practice | – | [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] |
| `agent-teammate-ignored-fields` | Agent used as a teammate: `skills` is never applied; `mcpServers` is ignored in-process; a teammate spawning an agent with `background: true` gets an error. (heuristic: teammate use is runtime) | off | warn | no-op | – | [^agent-teams-use-subagent-definitions-for-teammates] [^agent-teams-limitations] |
| `agent-teams-no-project-config` | No files under `.claude/teams/`: there is no project-level team config; such a file is an ordinary file. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^agent-teams-architecture] |
| `output-style-frontmatter-valid` | Line 1 is `---` and the YAML parses. A parse failure loads the style under its file name with no fields set. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^output-styles-frontmatter-reference] [^glossary-frontmatter] |
| `output-style-frontmatter-schema` | Keys in {`name`, `description`, `keep-coding-instructions`, `force-for-plugin`}, exact kebab-case (flag `keepCodingInstructions`). `keep-coding-instructions`, `force-for-plugin` are booleans. `force-for-plugin` only in plugin styles. Misspelled keys are ignored. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^output-styles-frontmatter-reference] [^claude-directory-frontmatter-fields-by-file] |
| `output-style-plugin-name-description` | Plugin output style sets `name` and `description` frontmatter. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-components-themes-and-output-styles] |
| `output-style-name-unique` | Effective style name (`name`, else file name) is unique across nested project `.claude/output-styles/` directories; the closest to the working directory wins. (cross-file) | recommended | warn | consistency | – | [^output-styles-create-a-custom-output-style] [^output-styles-frontmatter-reference] |
| `output-style-force-for-plugin` | Plugin style sets `force-for-plugin: true`: it overrides each user's `outputStyle`. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^output-styles-frontmatter-reference] |
| `output-style-force-for-plugin-unique` | At most one style across the plugins of a marketplace sets `force-for-plugin: true`; with several, the first loaded wins. (cross-file) | recommended | error | consistency | – | [^output-styles-frontmatter-reference] |
| `output-style-keep-coding-instructions` | Custom style omits `keep-coding-instructions: true`, so the built-in software engineering instructions are dropped. (heuristic: matters only for coding sessions) | off | warn | practice | – | [^output-styles-how-output-styles-work] [^output-styles-frontmatter-reference] |

#### Subagents and output styles: notes

- Scope difference for broken frontmatter. A local agent with unparsable YAML, no `name`, a `name` but no `description`, a bad `name`, or a late `---` is skipped silently [^sub-agents-subagent-files-claude-code-skips]. A plugin agent with the same fault still loads under its file name with a placeholder description and every field ignored [^plugins-components-frontmatter-fields-in-plugin-agents]. So `agent-frontmatter-valid` (category `load`) applies only to local agents, and `agent-plugin-frontmatter-valid` (category `no-op`) only to plugin agents. A plugin agent without `name` is valid. An output style with bad YAML also loads under its file name [^output-styles-frontmatter-reference].
- `agent-frontmatter-schema` sub-checks: `tools`, `disallowedTools` comma string or list; `model` string; `permissionMode` in `default`, `acceptEdits`, `auto`, `dontAsk`, `bypassPermissions`, `plan`, `manual`; `maxTurns` positive integer; `skills` string list; `memory` in `user`, `project`, `local`; `background`, `omitClaudeMd` booleans; `effort` in `low`, `medium`, `high`, `xhigh`, `max`; `isolation` = `worktree`; `color` in `red`, `blue`, `green`, `yellow`, `purple`, `orange`, `pink`, `cyan`; `experimental` a map with only `cacheTtl` in `5m`, `1h`; no top-level `cacheTtl` [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference] [^plugins-components-frontmatter-fields-in-plugin-agents].
- `maxTurns` type is not stated in the frontmatter table. Only the SDK table gives `number` [^agent-sdk-subagents-agentdefinition-configuration]. `effort` as a number is SDK-only and not accepted here.
- `isolation: worktree` as the only value is stated for plugin agents [^plugins-components-frontmatter-fields-in-plugin-agents]. The subagent table says only "Set to `worktree`" [^sub-agents-frontmatter-reference]. The stricter value applies to all scopes.
- Plugin ignored fields: the scope Note lists three (`hooks`, `mcpServers`, `permissionMode`) [^sub-agents-choose-the-subagent-scope]. The `initialPrompt` row and the plugin page add `initialPrompt` [^plugins-components-frontmatter-fields-in-plugin-agents]. The four-field list is used. The hooks group's `hooks-plugin-agent-hooks-ignored` duplicates the `hooks` part. Keep only one of the two enabled.
- Validate: the changelog says `claude plugin validate` checks agent frontmatter for "YAML parse errors and schema violations" (v2.1.77) [^changelog-claude-code-changelog]. The docs do not list which schema checks it runs, so only the parse checks are marked `covered`. Validate never reads `.claude/agents` unless named. It does not read output styles beyond the manifest path checks.
- `agent-tools-known` uses the permissions group's rule syntax for specifiers and the mcp-lsp group's canonical built-in tool list. An unrecognized entry is reported. When every entry fails, Claude Code refuses to launch the agent. An empty `tools` list launches with no tools and no error [^errors-agent-would-be-spawned-with-zero-tools].
- Background set for `agent-tools-unavailable` and `agent-tools-conditional`: `Read`, `Grep`, `Glob`, `LSP`, `Bash`, `PowerShell`, `Edit`, `Write`, `NotebookEdit`, `WebFetch`, `WebSearch`, `TodoWrite`, `Skill`, `ToolSearch`, `EnterWorktree`, `ExitWorktree`, `Monitor`, `TaskStop`, `SendMessage`, `Artifact`, `SubagentHandback`, plus MCP tools, `Agent` and `ExitPlanMode` [^sub-agents-available-tools].
- `agent-permission-mode-bypass` is `error`: on current versions the value is silently ignored, and before v2.1.267 it granted bypass [^sub-agents-permission-modes].
- `agent-memory-grants-write` is `error` because `memory` grants Write and Edit that the `tools` allowlist left out [^sub-agents-enable-persistent-memory].
- `agent-name-shadows-builtin`: overriding `Explore` with a cheaper `model` is a documented technique [^sub-agents-built-in-subagents]. Hence the `allow` option. `fork` from the phase 1 row is not a built-in agent name in the docs, so it is left out.
- `settings-teammate-mode` and `settings-teammate-default-model` are here as agent teams config. The docs put `teammateMode` in `~/.claude/settings.json` [^agent-teams-choose-a-display-mode]. `teammateDefaultModel` is `error`, not a deprecation `warn`, because Claude Code ignores the value [^agent-teams-specify-teammates-and-models].
- The 15,000-token limit counts each agent's `name` plus `description`, and has no documented char proxy [^errors-agent-descriptions-are-over-the-150k-token-limit]. So `agent-descriptions-budget` is `off`.
- Two headings used here are HTML `<h3>` tags, not `#` lines: sub-agents "Frontmatter reference" (anchor `supported-frontmatter-fields`) and output-styles "Frontmatter reference" (anchor `frontmatter`). The same holds for the two errors.md headings and the troubleshooting heading.
- Rows handed to other groups: frontmatter `hooks` shape, `Stop` to `SubagentStop`, hook script executable, `shell: powershell`, frontmatter-hook trust, `SubagentStart`/`SubagentStop` matchers (hooks). `agent-memory/*/MEMORY.md` size, memory dir orphans, `agent-memory-local/` gitignore (memory). Plugin `settings.json` `agent` and allowed keys, manifest `agents`/`outputStyles` replacing the folder scan (plugin). Settings `agent`, `outputStyle` case and resolution, `subagentPromptCacheTtl`, and env `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL`, `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENT_SPAWN_DEPTH`, `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_CONCURRENT_SUBAGENTS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_FORK_SUBAGENT`, `CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_AGENT_TEAMS` (settings). `Agent(name)` deny rules and `Agent(model:...)` (permissions). Glob/Grep with Bash in `tools` (mcp-lsp).
- Open question: the scope table ranks project agents over plugin agents by name [^sub-agents-choose-the-subagent-scope], but plugin agents register under `<plugin>:<name>`. The docs do not say whether a bare-name clash between them shadows anything, so no rule checks it.

### Hooks

These rules cover every hook config a repo can commit: the `hooks` key in `.claude/settings.json` and `.claude/settings.local.json`, `<plugin>/hooks/hooks.json`, the inline `hooks` in `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, the `hooks:` frontmatter in SKILL.md and agent `.md` files, and the script files that hook commands run. There are 62 rules: 22 `recommended` error, 18 `recommended` warn, 2 `bje` error, 1 `bje` warn, and 19 `off`.

Terms. A *group* is one object in an event's array (`matcher` and `hooks`). A *handler* is one object in a group's inner `hooks` array. *Shell form* is a `command` handler with no `args`. *Exec form* is a handler with `args`. *Placeholder* means `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}`, `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}` or `${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}`. Unless a row says otherwise, a rule applies to all five sources.

| Rule | Checks | Preset | Severity | Category | Validate | Docs |
|------|--------|--------|----------|----------|----------|------|
| `hooks-config-schema` | Event to groups to handlers shape; `matcher` a string (an array rejects the whole settings file); `type` enum; required field per type (`command`, `url`, `server`+`tool`, `prompt`); field types and `shell` enum (appendix). `hooks.json`: strict JSON, top-level keys `hooks` (required), `description`, `$schema`. | recommended | error | load | covered (plugin `hooks/hooks.json` only) | [^hooks-configuration] [^hooks-common-fields] [^hooks-command-hook-fields] [^hooks-http-hook-fields] [^hooks-mcp-tool-hook-fields] [^hooks-prompt-and-agent-hook-fields] [^hooks-reference-scripts-by-path] [^settings-reference-hooks] [^debug-your-config-check-hooks] [^hooks-guide-hooks-shows-no-hooks-configured] [^plugins-troubleshooting-hooks-fail-to-load] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `hooks-event-name-known` | Each key under `hooks` is one of the 33 documented event names (appendix), case-sensitive. Report a case or spelling near-miss (`preToolUse`, `pre_tool_use`) with the correct name. Claude Code skips an unknown event entry. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^hooks-hook-lifecycle] [^agent-sdk-hooks-hook-not-firing] [^plugins-troubleshooting-hook-loads-but-never-fires] [^settings-fix-a-broken-settings-file] |
| `hooks-no-standalone-file` | No `.claude/hooks.json` or `.claude/hooks/hooks.json` outside a plugin: project and user hooks go under the `hooks` key in settings. In a plugin, the file is `<plugin-root>/hooks/hooks.json`, not under `.claude-plugin/`. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^debug-your-config-check-common-causes] [^plugins-components-explore-the-plugin-directory] |
| `hooks-handler-field-unknown` | A handler holds only the fields documented for its `type` (list in appendix). | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-hook-handler-fields] |
| `hooks-handler-field-ignored` | Flags fields that have no effect: `async`/`asyncRewake` on non-`command`; `continueOnBlock` on non-`prompt`, or on `PostToolUseFailure`/`TaskCreated`; `shell` with `args`; `once` outside SKILL.md frontmatter; `timeout` with `async: true`; SessionEnd `timeout` > 60; plugin SessionEnd `timeout` > 1.5. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^hooks-common-fields] [^hooks-command-hook-fields] [^hooks-configure-an-async-hook] [^hooks-agent-hook-configuration] [^hooks-response-schema] [^hooks-sessionend-input] |
| `hooks-handler-type-event-support` | Handler `type` is one the event supports: `SessionStart` only `command`/`mcp_tool`, `Setup` only `command`; `mcp_tool` on `SessionStart` with matcher `startup`, `resume` or omitted is skipped; no `prompt`/`agent` on the events in the appendix; no `agent` on `PermissionRequest`; no `prompt`/`agent` on `PermissionDenied`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^hooks-prompt-based-hooks] [^hooks-setup-decision-control] [^hooks-events-that-fire-before-mcp-servers-are-available] [^hooks-response-schema] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `hooks-prompt-on-permission-request` | A `prompt` handler on `PermissionRequest` cannot deny (`ok: false` has no effect). Use `command` or `http`. | recommended | warn | no-op | – | [^hooks-response-schema] |
| `hooks-if-condition` | `if` only on `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`, `PostToolUseFailure`, `PermissionRequest`, `PermissionDenied` (else the handler never runs); exactly one rule, no `&&`, `\|\|`, comma or array; permission-rule syntax (permissions group rule); its tool is matched by the group `matcher`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^hooks-common-fields] [^hooks-how-a-hook-resolves] [^tools-reference-configure-tools-with-permission-rules-and-hooks] |
| `hooks-matcher-unsupported-event` | No `matcher` on an event without matcher support: `UserPromptSubmit`, `PostToolBatch`, `Stop`, `TeammateIdle`, `TaskCreated`, `TaskCompleted`, `WorktreeCreate`, `WorktreeRemove`, `MessageDisplay`, `CwdChanged`. Claude Code ignores it. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^hooks-matcher-patterns] |
| `hooks-matcher-syntax` | A matcher with a character outside `[A-Za-z0-9_\- ,\|]` compiles as a JavaScript RegExp; no `Tool(spec)` form; on `FileChanged` and `StopFailure` only `\|` separates, with no `,`, space or `-`; `FileChanged` segments hold no regex metacharacters (watched literally). | recommended | error | load | – | [^hooks-matcher-patterns] [^hooks-filechanged] [^tools-reference-configure-tools-with-permission-rules-and-hooks] |
| `hooks-matcher-mcp-name` | An MCP matcher has the form `mcp__<server>__<tool>`. A bare `mcp__<server>` with only exact-match characters matches no tool; write `mcp__<server>__.*`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^hooks-match-mcp-tools] |
| `hooks-matcher-never-matches` | A tool-event matcher segment is not a case-only variant of a built-in tool name (`bash`, `write`), not `EndConversation`, and not `Advisor`/`advisor`. None of these ever fires. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^hooks-guide-hook-not-firing] [^debug-your-config-check-common-causes] [^hooks-pretooluse] [^tools-reference-check-which-tools-are-available] |
| `hooks-matcher-enum` | For events with a fixed matcher set (`SessionStart`, `Setup`, `SessionEnd`, `Notification`, `PreCompact`, `PostCompact`, `ConfigChange`, `DirectoryAdded`, `StopFailure`, `InstructionsLoaded`), each exact-set segment is a documented value (appendix). | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^hooks-matcher-patterns] |
| `hooks-matcher-deprecated-value` | `SessionEnd` matcher does not use `bypass_permissions_disabled` (removed in v2.1.234). | recommended | warn | deprecated | – | [^hooks-sessionend] |
| `hooks-matcher-legacy-version` | Only when option `minVersion` is below the named version: anchor hyphenated names (`^code-reviewer$`) before 2.1.195; use `\|` not `,` before 2.1.191; no `StopFailure` `cloud_credential_error` before 2.1.267; no Notification `quota_auto_resume_*` before 2.1.234. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^hooks-matcher-patterns] [^debug-your-config-check-hooks] [^hooks-notification] |
| `hooks-matcher-bash-without-powershell` | A tool-event matcher that names `Bash` also names `PowerShell` (`Bash\|PowerShell`), so shell hooks also cover Windows. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^hooks-powershell] [^tools-reference-powershell-tool] |
| `hooks-matcher-subagent-anchor` | A `SubagentStart`/`SubagentStop` matcher that holds `:` (plugin-scoped agent) starts with `^` and ends with `$`. The colon makes it an unanchored regex. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-subagentstart] [^sub-agents-project-level-hooks-for-subagent-events] |
| `hooks-filechanged-star-matcher` | No `"*"` matcher on `FileChanged`: it also registers a literal file named `*`. Omit the matcher for a match-all group. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-filechanged] |
| `hooks-placeholder-quoted` | In shell form, each placeholder sits inside double quotes, unless `shell` is `"powershell"`. An unquoted one breaks when the path holds a space. Both `"${X}/a.sh"` and `"${X}"/a.sh` pass. | recommended | warn | portability | covered (plugin hooks files only, warning) | [^hooks-reference-scripts-by-path] [^plugins-manifest-reference-quoting-and-path-separators] [^plugins-troubleshooting-hook-error-notices-in-the-transcript] |
| `hooks-prefer-exec-form` | A `command` handler that references a placeholder sets `args` (exec form). | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-reference-scripts-by-path] [^hooks-guide-hook-json-has-no-effect] |
| `hooks-exec-form-command-spaces` | In exec form, a bare `command` (no path separator) holds no whitespace. The spawn fails. | recommended | error | load | – | [^hooks-exec-form-and-shell-form] |
| `hooks-powershell-placeholder` | With `shell: "powershell"`: no bare `$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` (resolves to `$null`); no placeholder inside single quotes (not expanded). | recommended | error | load | – | [^hooks-windows-powershell-tool] |
| `hooks-ps1-needs-powershell-shell` | A `command` that runs a `.ps1` file sets `shell: "powershell"`. Condition: fails on Windows. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^sub-agents-conditional-rules-with-hooks] |
| `hooks-script-exists` | (cross-file) A script path in `command` or `args` that resolves inside the repo after placeholder resolution exists. A mistyped path leaves the hook silently off. | recommended | error | load | – | [^hooks-other-exit-codes] [^hooks-guide-hook-error-in-output] |
| `hooks-script-executable` | (cross-file) A repo script that is itself the `command` (not `node x.ts`, `bash x.sh`) has git mode `100755`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^hooks-guide-block-edits-to-protected-files] [^sub-agents-conditional-rules-with-hooks] [^plugins-components-hooks] |
| `hooks-env-var-unavailable` | An inline `command` or `args` references `CLAUDE_ENV_FILE` only on `SessionStart`, `Setup`, `CwdChanged`, `FileChanged`, and never references `$CLAUDE_MODEL` (does not exist). | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^hooks-persist-environment-variables] [^hooks-common-input-fields] |
| `hooks-http-env-allowlist` | (cross-file) Each `$VAR`/`${VAR}` in an `http` handler's `headers` is in its `allowedEnvVars`, and in settings `httpHookAllowedEnvVars` when that key is set. Else it becomes an empty string. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^hooks-http-hook-fields] [^hooks-hook-locations] |
| `hooks-http-url-allowlist` | (cross-file) When settings `allowedHttpHookUrls` is set, each `http` handler `url` matches one of its patterns. An empty array blocks every HTTP hook. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^hooks-hook-locations] [^settings-reference-allowedhttphookurls] |
| `hooks-disabled-by-disableallhooks` | A settings file does not set `disableAllHooks: true` and also define `hooks`. The hooks never run. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^settings-reference-disableallhooks] |
| `hooks-disable-all-override` | Committed `.claude/settings.json` does not set `disableAllHooks: false`. It overrides a user's `true` and turns the user's hooks back on. | recommended | warn | security | – | [^hooks-disable-or-remove-hooks] [^permissions-what-runs-before-you-trust-a-folder] |
| `hooks-committed-command-review` | Reports each committed command hook (project settings, plugin hooks, agent frontmatter) for review: it runs with full user permissions, outside the sandbox, and without a trust dialog in `-p`/SDK sessions. | off | warn | security | – | [^hooks-workspace-trust] [^hooks-disclaimer] [^plugins-security-understand-what-a-plugin-can-do] [^permissions-what-runs-before-you-trust-a-folder] |
| `hooks-sessionend-default-timeout` | A non-plugin `SessionEnd` handler sets `timeout`. Without it the handler is cancelled after the 1.5 s default. | recommended | warn | limit | – | [^hooks-sessionend-input] |
| `hooks-duplicate-handler` | (cross-file) No identical handler (same event, matcher, `type`, command) in both a plugin's `hooks/hooks.json` and its `plugin.json` `hooks`, or in both `.claude/settings*.json` and a plugin `hooks.json`. It runs twice. | recommended | warn | consistency | – | [^plugins-components-hooks] [^plugins-create-convert-an-existing-claude-setup] |
| `hooks-worktree-create-without-remove` | A `WorktreeCreate` hook has a `WorktreeRemove` hook in the same source. Without one, the worktree directory stays on disk. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-worktreeremove] |
| `hooks-agent-stop-event` | Agent `.md` frontmatter uses `SubagentStop`, not `Stop`. Claude Code converts `Stop` to `SubagentStop` there. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-hooks-in-skills-and-agents] [^sub-agents-hooks-in-subagent-frontmatter] |
| `hooks-agent-type-experimental` | No `type: "agent"` handler: agent hooks are experimental. Prefer `command`. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-agent-based-hooks] |
| `hooks-command-removed-cli-flag` | A hook `command`/`args` that runs `claude` does not pass `--enable-auto-mode` (removed in v2.1.111). | recommended | error | load | – | [^cli-reference-cli-flags] |
| `hooks-command-deprecated-cli-flag` | A hook that runs `claude` uses `--cloud`, not `--remote`. Condition, self-hosted runner: use `--system-prompt-file`/`--append-system-prompt-file`, not `--system-prompt`/`--append-system-prompt`. | recommended | warn | deprecated | – | [^cli-reference-cli-flags] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `hooks-bje-sessionstart-shape` | A plugin `SessionStart` dependency-check group has matcher `startup\|resume\|clear\|compact` and handler `timeout` 3 or less (`max` 3). | bje | error | practice | – | [^hooks-sessionstart] [^hooks-common-fields] |
| `hooks-bje-node-guard` | A plugin hook `command` that runs a `.ts` file with `node` checks `command -v node` in the `hooks.json` command string and writes `systemMessage` JSON when node is missing. | bje | error | practice | – | [^hooks-json-output] |
| `hooks-command-path-variable` | (heuristic) A plugin hook reaches bundled files through `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}`, and a project hook through `${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}`, not a bare relative, cwd-relative or absolute path. | bje | warn | portability | – | [^plugins-components-reference-plugin-paths-and-store-data] [^hooks-guide-hook-error-in-output] |
| `hooks-async-on-blocking-event` | (heuristic) `async: true` on an event whose output blocks or decides (`PreToolUse`, `PermissionRequest`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `Stop`, `SubagentStop`, `ConfigChange`, `PreModelSwitch`, `WorktreeCreate`). Async output cannot block. Logging hooks are a valid use. | off | warn | no-op | – | [^hooks-run-hooks-in-the-background] |
| `hooks-timeout-units` | (heuristic) `timeout` is in seconds; a value of 1000 or more is likely milliseconds. | off | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-common-fields] |
| `hooks-matcher-unanchored-regex` | (heuristic) A regex matcher that starts with a known tool name and has no `^`/`$` (`Edit.*` also hits `NotebookEdit`). | off | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-matcher-patterns] |
| `hooks-matcher-name-unresolved` | (heuristic) A tool-event matcher names a canonical tool (mcp-lsp appendix) or `mcp__*`; a Subagent matcher names a known agent `name` or plugin-scoped id; an `mcp_tool` `server` names a server configured in the repo. | off | warn | no-op | – | [^debug-your-config-check-hooks] [^sub-agents-project-level-hooks-for-subagent-events] [^hooks-mcp-tool-hook-fields] |
| `hooks-if-dir-glob-depth` | (heuristic) A single-segment `Tool(dir/**)` in `if` matches only `<cwd>/dir` since v2.1.214; suggest `**/dir/**` for any depth. | off | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-common-fields] |
| `hooks-filechanged-needs-watch-seed` | (heuristic) `FileChanged` groups exist but none names a file, and no `SessionStart`/`CwdChanged` hook returns `watchPaths`. The watcher never starts. | off | warn | no-op | – | [^hooks-filechanged] |
| `hooks-exec-form-shell-syntax` | (heuristic) In exec form, `command`/`args` hold no shell syntax (`\|`, `&&`, `>`, globs, `$VAR`). There is no shell, so they pass through as literal text. | off | warn | load | – | [^hooks-exec-form-and-shell-form] |
| `hooks-exec-form-windows-shim` | (heuristic) Exec-form `command` is not an npm `.cmd`/`.bat` shim (`npx`, `npm`, `eslint`, `node_modules/.bin/*`). Condition: fails on Windows. | off | warn | portability | – | [^hooks-exec-form-and-shell-form] |
| `hooks-script-shebang` | (heuristic) A script run directly as `command` starts with `#!`. | off | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-guide-block-edits-to-protected-files] |
| `hooks-http-literal-secret` | (heuristic) `http` handler `headers` values use `$VAR` interpolation, not a literal token. | off | warn | security | – | [^hooks-http-hook-fields] |
| `hooks-broad-auto-approve` | (heuristic) A handler that returns allow (`PermissionRequest` `behavior: "allow"`, `PreToolUse` `permissionDecision: "allow"`) under an empty, `*` or `.*` matcher; any `setMode` to `bypassPermissions`. | off | warn | security | – | [^hooks-guide-auto-approve-specific-permission-prompts] [^hooks-pretooluse-decision-control] [^hooks-permission-update-entries] |
| `hooks-command-shell-safety` | (heuristic) Shell-form commands and repo scripts quote shell variables (`"$VAR"`), and do not run destructive commands (`rm -rf`) on input-derived paths. | off | warn | security | – | [^hooks-security-best-practices] [^hooks-disclaimer] |
| `hooks-output-blocking-exit-code` | (heuristic) A script on a blocking event that means to block uses `exit 2`, not `exit 1`. | off | warn | load | – | [^hooks-other-exit-codes] |
| `hooks-output-schema` | (heuristic) Literal JSON output: `hookSpecificOutput.hookEventName` equals the event; `permissionDecision`, `additionalContext`, `updatedInput` sit inside `hookSpecificOutput`; `decision` is only `"block"`; no `suppressOutput`; `PreModelSwitch` has no `defer`, `updatedInput`, `additionalContext`; no `classifierContext` from async hooks; no block on `ConfigChange` `policy_settings`. | off | warn | no-op | – | [^hooks-json-output] [^hooks-guide-hook-json-has-no-effect] [^hooks-decision-control] [^hooks-premodelswitch-decision-control] [^hooks-annotate-a-result-for-the-auto-mode-classifier] [^hooks-configchange-decision-control] |
| `hooks-output-deprecated-fields` | (heuristic) Literal output or script does not use `PreToolUse` top-level `decision`/`reason` (`approve`/`block`), or `updatedMCPToolOutput` (use `updatedToolOutput`), and does not read input field `team_name`. | off | warn | deprecated | – | [^hooks-pretooluse-decision-control] [^hooks-posttooluse-decision-control] [^hooks-taskcreated-input] |
| `hooks-output-size` | (heuristic) Literal `additionalContext`, `systemMessage`, `initialUserMessage` or plain stdout `max` 10,000 chars (else a file path and 2,000-char preview); `classifierContext` `max` 2,000 chars per tool call. | off | warn | limit | – | [^hooks-json-output] [^hooks-annotate-a-result-for-the-auto-mode-classifier] |
| `hooks-stop-loop-guard` | (heuristic) A `Stop`/`SubagentStop` script that blocks reads `stop_hook_active` and exits early when it is `true`. | off | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-guide-stop-hook-hits-the-block-cap] |
| `hooks-multiple-updatedinput` | (heuristic) At most one `PreToolUse` handler for the same tool returns `updatedInput`. The last to finish wins. | off | warn | consistency | – | [^hooks-guide-limitations] |
| `hooks-sessionstart-static-context` | (heuristic) A `SessionStart` command that only echoes static text belongs in CLAUDE.md; one that prints `AGENTS.md` adds a second copy to context. | off | warn | practice | – | [^hooks-add-context-for-claude] [^memory-remove-an-earlier-agents-md-workaround] |

#### Appendix: value sets

- Events (33): `SessionStart`, `Setup`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `UserPromptExpansion`, `PreToolUse`, `PermissionRequest`, `PermissionDenied`, `PostToolUse`, `PostToolUseFailure`, `PostToolBatch`, `Notification`, `MessageDisplay`, `SubagentStart`, `SubagentStop`, `TaskCreated`, `TaskCompleted`, `Stop`, `StopFailure`, `TeammateIdle`, `InstructionsLoaded`, `ConfigChange`, `CwdChanged`, `DirectoryAdded`, `FileChanged`, `WorktreeCreate`, `WorktreeRemove`, `PreCompact`, `PostCompact`, `PreModelSwitch`, `PostModelSwitch`, `Elicitation`, `ElicitationResult`, `SessionEnd`.
- Handler fields. Common: `type`, `if`, `timeout`, `statusMessage`, `once`. `command`: `command`, `args`, `async`, `asyncRewake`, `shell`. `http`: `url`, `headers`, `allowedEnvVars`. `mcp_tool`: `server`, `tool`, `input`. `prompt`: `prompt`, `model`, `continueOnBlock`. `agent`: `prompt`, `model`.
- Handler `type`: `command`, `http`, `mcp_tool`, `prompt`, `agent`. `shell`: `bash`, `powershell`. Field types: `timeout` number; `async`, `asyncRewake`, `once`, `continueOnBlock` boolean; `args`, `allowedEnvVars` string arrays; `headers`, `input` objects; `statusMessage`, `if`, `prompt`, `model` strings.
- No `prompt`/`agent` handlers: `ConfigChange`, `CwdChanged`, `DirectoryAdded`, `Elicitation`, `ElicitationResult`, `FileChanged`, `InstructionsLoaded`, `MessageDisplay`, `Notification`, `PostCompact`, `PostModelSwitch`, `PreCompact`, `PreModelSwitch`, `SessionEnd`, `StopFailure`, `SubagentStart`, `WorktreeCreate`, `WorktreeRemove` (plus `SessionStart`, `Setup`, which also forbid `http`).
- Matcher sets. `SessionStart`: `startup`, `resume`, `clear`, `compact`, `fork`. `Setup`: `init`, `maintenance`. `SessionEnd`: `clear`, `resume`, `logout`, `prompt_input_exit`, `other`. `Notification`: `permission_prompt`, `idle_prompt`, `auth_success`, `elicitation_dialog`, `elicitation_url_dialog`, `elicitation_complete`, `elicitation_response`, `agent_needs_input`, `agent_completed`, `quota_auto_resume_fired`, `quota_auto_resume_stale`, `quota_auto_resume_disabled`. `PreCompact`/`PostCompact`: `manual`, `auto`. `ConfigChange`: `user_settings`, `project_settings`, `local_settings`, `policy_settings`, `skills`. `DirectoryAdded`: `slash_command`, `register_repo_root`. `StopFailure`: `rate_limit`, `overloaded`, `authentication_failed`, `oauth_org_not_allowed`, `account_on_hold`, `billing_error`, `invalid_request`, `model_not_found`, `server_error`, `max_output_tokens`, `cloud_credential_error`, `unknown`. `InstructionsLoaded`: `session_start`, `nested_traversal`, `path_glob_match`, `include`, `compact`.
- `"*"`, `""` and an omitted matcher all mean match-all (except `FileChanged`, see `hooks-filechanged-star-matcher`).

#### Hooks: notes
- Validate scope. The docs say `claude plugin validate` checks `hooks/hooks.json` for JSON and schema errors (changelog v2.1.77) and warns on an unquoted placeholder in a shell-form hook (v2.1.281). Nothing else about hooks is stated. Validate never reads settings `hooks` or frontmatter `hooks` in project `.claude/`. [^plugins-troubleshooting-hooks-fail-to-load] [^plugins-manifest-reference-quoting-and-path-separators] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog]
- Changelog footnotes point at the page title, because `changelog.md` has no per-version headings. The versions cited: 2.1.77 (validate hooks.json), 2.1.101 (unknown event skips one entry), 2.1.142 (prompt/agent on `SessionStart`/`Setup`/`SubagentStart` is an error), 2.1.274 (`$schema` in hooks.json), 2.1.280 (agent on `PermissionRequest` is an error), 2.1.281 (runner system-prompt files). [^changelog-claude-code-changelog]
- Conflicts, stricter value chosen. `agent` on `PermissionRequest`: hooks.md says "skips it", the changelog (2.1.280) says it "shows an error". Both are error severity. `allowedHttpHookUrls` mismatch: settings-reference says it blocks the hook (breaks), hooks.md says it does not run (no-op). Both give error. [^hooks-prompt-based-hooks] [^settings-reference-allowedhttphookurls]
- Executable bit. sub-agents.md limits this to macOS and Linux, and hooks-guide.md says "Hook scripts must be executable" with no condition. I chose the stricter hooks-guide wording (error), scoped to scripts run directly as `command`. [^hooks-guide-block-edits-to-protected-files] [^sub-agents-conditional-rules-with-hooks]
- Placeholder quoting is warn, not error. The failure needs a space in the install or project path (troubleshooting). The plugins-marketplaces findings called it breaks. [^plugins-troubleshooting-hook-error-notices-in-the-transcript]
- `once`. hooks.md says it is ignored "in settings files and agent frontmatter" and does not name plugin `hooks.json`. The rule flags settings and agent frontmatter only. [^hooks-common-fields]
- Unknown handler fields. The docs list the fields per type but do not say what happens to an unknown one, so `hooks-handler-field-unknown` is warn, not error. [^hooks-hook-handler-fields]
- `hooks-committed-command-review` flags every committed command hook, including this repo's own plugin hooks. The policy table puts it in `recommended` warn. It is a candidate to move to `off`, because it reports no misconfiguration. [^hooks-workspace-trust]
- `async: true` on a decision event was an exact finding (degraded). I moved it to `off` as a heuristic, because an async logging hook on `PreToolUse` is valid, and the docs name no error. [^hooks-run-hooks-in-the-background]
- The bje rules come from `.claude/rules/_design-principles.md` ("Dependencies outside a plugin", "State"), not from the docs. Their footnotes point at the nearest docs heading for the fields that they check. `hooks-command-path-variable` is a heuristic under the docs policy (`off`); bje turns it on because "A literal path ... is a defect". [^hooks-sessionstart] [^plugins-components-reference-plugin-paths-and-store-data]
- Profile echo. hooks-guide.md says a shell profile `echo` can corrupt a shell-form hook's JSON. This is a second reason for `hooks-prefer-exec-form`. The repo cannot see the profile, so there is no separate rule. [^hooks-guide-hook-json-has-no-effect]
- `shell: "powershell"` on a hook works without `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_POWERSHELL_TOOL`, unlike `defaultShell` and skill `shell`. So `hooks-config-schema` needs no env-var check. [^tools-reference-shell-selection-in-settings-hooks-and-skills]
- Left to other groups: `hooks` in plugin agent frontmatter is ignored (agents: plugin-agent ignored fields). Marketplace entry `hooks` as a path or array, and entry matchers that replace manifest matchers (marketplace). Plugin.json `hooks` path not found, `../` escapes, and no state writes under `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}` (plugin). `httpHookAllowedEnvVars` type, `allowManagedHooksOnly` managed-only, `statusLine` under `disableAllHooks`, `hooks` in the security-approval key list, nested `.claude/settings.json` without parent fallback, multi-repo cloud keys (settings). Permission-rule grammar for `if`, and transcript labels such as `Stop Task` in matchers (permissions). Canonical tool list (mcp-lsp appendix).
- Dropped rows are listed in the report to the caller. They are the timeout defaults reference row (no check) and the match-all forms preference row (the docs state that the forms are equivalent and name no preferred form).

### Plugin manifest and layout

Covers `.claude-plugin/plugin.json` (every field except inline `mcpServers`, `lspServers` and `hooks`), the plugin directory layout, `bin/`, symlinks, component path rules, plugin `settings.json`, `userConfig`, `dependencies`, `channels`, `monitors`, `themes`, Node.js lockfiles, marketplace-entry `relevance`, and plugin eval suites. 83 rules: 74 plugin rules plus 9 eval-suite rules in their own subsection.

| Rule | Checks | Preset | Severity | Category | Validate | Docs |
|------|--------|--------|----------|----------|----------|------|
| `plugin-manifest-location` | Manifest is `<plugin-root>/.claude-plugin/plugin.json`. `.claude-plugin/` holds only `plugin.json` and `marketplace.json`; flag `skills/`, `commands/`, `agents/`, `hooks/`, `.mcp.json` or other components inside it (never scanned). | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-manifest-file] [^plugins-create-plugin-layout] [^plugins-troubleshooting-plugin-loads-but-its-skills-are-missing] |
| `plugin-manifest-valid-json` | `plugin.json` parses as JSON (`corrupt manifest file` otherwise). | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-troubleshooting-plugin-name-has-a-corrupt-manifest-file-or-has-an-invalid-manifest-file] |
| `plugin-manifest-no-bom` | `plugin.json` has no UTF-8 byte-order mark. Condition: install fails on Claude Code before v2.1.246 (changelog 2.1.246). | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `plugin-manifest-schema` | Field types: `name`, `displayName`, `version`, `description`, `license`, `repository` strings; `author` object with required non-empty `name`, optional `email`, `url`; `homepage` parses as URL; `keywords` string[]; `defaultEnabled` boolean; `metadata`, `experimental` objects. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-fields] [^plugins-manifest-reference-validate-the-manifest] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `plugin-manifest-name` | `name` present and non-empty; no spaces, `@`, `:`, `/`, `\`, control or bidirectional-formatting characters. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-name] [^plugins-manifest-reference-fields] |
| `plugin-manifest-name-kebab-case` | `name` matches `^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$`. Claude Code accepts other forms; claude.ai marketplace sync rejects them. | recommended | warn | portability | covered | [^plugins-troubleshooting-marketplace-validation-errors] [^plugins-publish-prepare-your-plugin-for-release] |
| `plugin-manifest-name-stable` | `name` equals its value at the previous commit, unless the marketplace `renames` map has an entry for the old name (cross-file, reads git history). | recommended | error | consistency | – | [^plugins-publish-rename-or-remove-a-plugin] |
| `plugin-manifest-unknown-field` | Top-level keys within: `$schema`, `name`, `displayName`, `version`, `description`, `author`, `homepage`, `repository`, `license`, `keywords`, `metadata`, `defaultEnabled`, `dependencies`, `settings`, `userConfig`, `channels`, `skills`, `commands`, `agents`, `hooks`, `mcpServers`, `lspServers`, `outputStyles`, `workflows`, `experimental`, `privacyPolicyUrl`, `supportUrl`. Unknown keys are stripped. | recommended | error | no-op | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-unrecognized-fields] [^plugins-manifest-reference-fields] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `plugin-manifest-description-author` | `description` and `author` present. validate warns; `--strict` fails. | recommended | warn | practice | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-validate-the-manifest] |
| `plugin-manifest-version-present` | `version` present. validate warns; `--strict` fails. Not in `recommended`: the hosting docs recommend no `version`, so that users track commits. `bje` sets `error` to match `--strict` in CI. | bje | error | practice | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-validate-the-manifest] [^plugins-publish-prepare-your-plugin-for-release] [^plugins-host-marketplace-release-a-new-version] |
| `plugin-manifest-version-semver` | When set, `version` is valid semver. Claude Code does not check it, but dependency ranges and `<name>--v<version>` tags need it. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-version] [^plugins-dependencies-create-a-release-tag] |
| `plugin-manifest-version-bump` | (heuristic) When `version` is set and files under the plugin changed since the last commit that changed `version`, flag a missing bump. Users stay on the cached copy until the string changes. | off | warn | no-op | – | [^plugins-loading-how-claude-code-computes-the-version] [^plugins-host-marketplace-release-a-new-version] |
| `plugin-release-tag-matches-version` | Release tags for the plugin read `<plugin-name>--v<version>`, and `<version>` equals `plugin.json` `version` at the tagged commit (cross-file, reads git tags). | recommended | warn | consistency | – | [^plugins-dependencies-create-a-release-tag] |
| `plugin-manifest-publish-metadata` | `homepage` and `repository` set in `plugin.json`, and `README.md` exists at the plugin root. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-publish-prepare-your-plugin-for-release] |
| `plugin-manifest-metadata-format` | `license` is an SPDX identifier. `repository` parses as a URL (Claude Code does not validate it). | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-fields] |
| `plugin-feature-min-version` | With option `minVersion` set, flag features newer than it: `skills: "."` (v2.1.221, use `"./"`), `metadata` (v2.1.222), any `userConfig` `options` (v2.1.271, plugin fails to load). Reports nothing without `minVersion`. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-path-rules] [^plugins-manifest-reference-metadata] [^plugins-manifest-reference-limit-a-field-to-fixed-options] |
| `plugin-no-root-claude-md` | No `CLAUDE.md` at the plugin root. It is not loaded as context. | recommended | error | no-op | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-standard-layout] [^plugins-cli-reference-validate-a-directory] |
| `plugin-skill-dir-layout` | Each skill under a scanned skills directory is `<dir>/<name>/SKILL.md`. Flag loose `.md` files directly in `skills/`. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-standard-layout] |
| `plugin-no-project-plugins-dir` | No plugin under a repository's `.claude/plugins/`. Claude Code never scans it; use `.claude/skills/` or `enabledPlugins`. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-loading-plugins-shared-through-a-repository] |
| `plugin-project-skills-dir-limits` | For `.claude/skills/*/.claude-plugin/plugin.json`: flag monitors, MCP servers declared as `.mcpb`/`.dxt` bundles or from files outside the plugin, and a `.claude/skills/` that is not at the repository root. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-loading-plugins-shared-through-a-repository] |
| `plugin-path-dot-slash` | Every component path in `plugin.json` starts with `./`. Exceptions: `skills` may be `"."`; `mcpServers` may be an `https://` bundle URL. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-path-rules] |
| `plugin-path-containment` | No component path contains `..` or resolves, through a symlink, outside the plugin root. Exception: a link to a sibling plugin in the same marketplace. Rejected paths are dropped. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-containment-and-existence] [^plugins-loading-paths-that-escape-the-plugin-directory] [^errors-path-escapes-plugin-directory] |
| `plugin-path-no-backslash` | No `\` in any component path. Condition: rejected on macOS and Linux, loads on Windows only. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^plugins-loading-paths-that-escape-the-plugin-directory] [^errors-path-escapes-plugin-directory] |
| `plugin-path-exists` | Each component path, and each default location (`skills/`, `monitors/monitors.json`, root `SKILL.md`), exists and resolves without a symlink loop (`ELOOP`). validate checks `outputStyles`, `lspServers`, `monitors`, `themes` paths from v2.1.283. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-containment-and-existence] [^errors-path-could-not-be-checked] |
| `plugin-path-kind` | `agents` entries are `.md` files, not directories. `skills` entries are directories, not a `SKILL.md` file. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-path-only-fields] [^plugins-troubleshooting-claude-plugin-validate-reports-errors] |
| `plugin-commands-map-source-xor-content` | In an object-map `commands`, each value sets exactly one of `source` or `content`. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-commands] |
| `plugin-commands-map-fields` | Object-map `commands` values use only `source`, `content`, `description`, `argumentHint`, `model`, `allowedTools`; `allowedTools` is a string array. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-commands] |
| `plugin-commands-dir-nonempty` | A custom `commands` path holds at least one `.md` file or a subdirectory with `SKILL.md`. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-troubleshooting-warning-no-commands-found-in-plugin-name-custom-directory] |
| `plugin-default-dir-shadowed` | When `commands`, `agents`, `outputStyles`, `workflows`, `experimental.themes` or `experimental.monitors` is set, the matching default folder exists, and the key points nowhere inside it, flag the ignored folder. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-how-each-key-combines-with-its-default-location] [^plugins-components-themes-and-output-styles] |
| `plugin-skills-key-redundant-default` | `skills` adds to the default `skills/` scan. Flag a `skills` entry that names `./skills` again. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-how-each-key-combines-with-its-default-location] |
| `plugin-experimental-top-level-keys` | `themes` and `monitors` belong under `experimental`. A top-level key still loads, with a validate warning. | recommended | warn | deprecated | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-fields] |
| `plugin-settings-keys` | Plugin `settings.json` and manifest `settings` set only `agent` and `subagentStatusLine`. Other keys are dropped at load. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-settings] [^plugins-components-default-settings] |
| `plugin-settings-single-source` | Do not set both a root `settings.json` with a supported key and manifest `settings`. The manifest `settings` is then ignored (cross-file). | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-components-default-settings] |
| `plugin-settings-agent-exists` | Plugin `settings` `agent` names one of the plugin's own agents (cross-file). | recommended | warn | consistency | – | [^plugins-components-default-settings] |
| `plugin-user-config-schema` | For `userConfig` and `channels[].userConfig`: keys match `^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$`; only documented fields; `type`, `title`, `description` required; `type` in `string`, `number`, `boolean`, `directory`, `file`; `default` string, number, boolean or string[]; `options` rules. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-user-configuration] [^plugins-manifest-reference-limit-a-field-to-fixed-options] [^plugins-manifest-reference-unrecognized-fields] |
| `plugin-user-config-field-applicability` | `min`/`max` only on `type: number`. `multiple` only on `type: string`. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-user-configuration] |
