| `plugin-user-config-ref-declared` | Every `${user_config.KEY}` in `.mcp.json`, `mcpServers`, `lspServers`, hook `args`, skill, agent and command bodies names a declared option (cross-file). validate covers MCP references only (v2.1.281+). | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-validate-the-manifest] [^plugins-manifest-reference-reference-a-saved-value] |
| `plugin-user-config-no-shell-fields` | No `${user_config.*}` in a shell-form hook `command` (no `args`), a monitor `command`, or an MCP `headersHelper`. The component fails instead of running. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-fields-that-run-through-a-shell] [^errors-plugin-command-references-user_config-in-a-shell-command] |
| `plugin-user-config-sensitive-in-content` | No `${user_config.KEY}` for a `sensitive` option in skill, agent or command Markdown. It becomes a placeholder (cross-file). | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-reference-a-saved-value] |
| `plugin-channels-schema` | Each `channels[]` entry uses only `server`, `displayName`, `userConfig`; `server` is required and matches a key in the plugin's `mcpServers` (cross-file). | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-channels] [^plugins-components-channels] |
| `plugin-channel-server-stdio` | (heuristic) The server a channel binds to is a stdio server (`command`), not a remote `url` server. | off | warn | load | – | [^channels-reference-overview] |
| `plugin-dependencies-schema` | Each `dependencies` item is `"name"`, `"name@marketplace"`, or `{name, marketplace?, version?}` with string values; `version` is a valid semver range. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-dependencies] [^plugins-troubleshooting-dependency-errors] |
| `plugin-dependencies-resolve` | Bare names exist in the same marketplace's `plugins[]`. A cross-marketplace target is in `allowCrossMarketplaceDependenciesOn`. A constrained dependency with an `npm`, `archive` or `command` source sets `version` in its `plugin.json` (cross-file). | recommended | error | consistency | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-dependencies] [^plugins-dependencies-depend-on-a-plugin-from-another-marketplace] [^plugins-dependencies-constrain-a-dependency-that-has-a-non-git-source] |
| `plugin-dependencies-not-auto-installed` | Flag a dependency whose marketplace entry uses a `command` source; Claude Code never installs it, so users install it first (cross-file). | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-dependencies-constrain-a-dependency-that-has-a-non-git-source] |
| `plugin-dependencies-prerelease-range` | (heuristic) A range that names a pre-release major (for example `^2.0.0` while the target is `2.0.0-beta.1`) needs the `-0` suffix to match pre-releases. | off | warn | no-op | – | [^plugins-dependencies-declare-a-dependency-with-a-version-constraint] |
| `plugin-monitors-schema` | `monitors/monitors.json` or `experimental.monitors` is an array. Entries use only `name`, `command`, `description`, `when`; first three required; `name` unique in the plugin; `when` is `always` or `on-skill-invoke:<skill>`. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-manifest-reference-monitors] [^plugins-manifest-reference-standard-layout] |
| `plugin-monitors-skill-exists` | `when: "on-skill-invoke:<skill>"` names a skill in the same plugin (cross-file). | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-monitors] |
| `plugin-monitors-command-env` | A monitor `command` does not read `$CLAUDE_PLUGIN_OPTION_*`, `$CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT` or `$CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA` from the environment (not exported). Use the inline `${...}` form. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-where-each-variable-resolves] [^plugins-components-monitors] |
| `plugin-monitors-command-quote` | `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}` and `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}` in a monitor `command` sit inside double quotes. Condition: breaks when the install path has spaces. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-quoting-and-path-separators] |
| `plugin-themes-layout` | Each theme is `themes/<slug>.json` in the custom theme format (`name`, `base`, `overrides`). | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-components-themes-and-output-styles] |
| `plugin-bin-executable` | Every file in `<plugin>/bin/` has the executable bit (git mode `100755`). | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugins-components-executables] |
| `plugin-bin-claude-ai` | Flag a top-level `bin/`. Condition: claude.ai and Cowork, including claude.ai org sync, do not install the plugin. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^plugins-components-executables] [^plugins-manifest-reference-standard-layout] |
| `plugin-bin-shadows-system-command` | (heuristic) A `bin/` file named like a system command (`git`, `ls`, ...) never runs; plugin `bin/` comes after the user's `PATH`. | off | warn | no-op | – | [^plugins-components-executables] |
| `plugin-path-var-braced` | In plugin skill, command and agent Markdown, write `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}` / `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}`, not bare `$CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT`. Bash tool commands do not have these variables; only the `${...}` form is substituted. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-where-each-variable-resolves] |
| `plugin-path-uses-plugin-root` | (heuristic) Hook, monitor, MCP and LSP commands name bundled files through `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/...`, not a bare relative or absolute path. | off | warn | load | – | [^plugins-publish-prepare-your-plugin-for-release] [^plugins-components-reference-plugin-paths-and-store-data] |
| `plugin-no-parent-path-escape` | (heuristic) Plugin scripts and commands do not read paths above the plugin root such as `../shared`. A copied plugin does not have them. | off | warn | load | – | [^plugins-loading-in-place-and-copied-plugins] [^plugins-troubleshooting-files-the-plugin-references-outside-its-directory-arent-found] |
| `plugin-no-state-in-plugin-root` | (heuristic) Commands and scripts do not write under `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}` (`>`, `>>`, `cp`, `npm install`); use `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}`. The root changes on update. | off | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-components-reference-plugin-paths-and-store-data] |
| `plugin-symlink-escapes-marketplace` | A symlink under a plugin resolves inside the marketplace root (inside the plugin root when no `marketplace.json` encloses it). A link outside is skipped on every install. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugins-host-marketplace-share-files-within-a-marketplace-with-symlinks] |
| `plugin-symlink-escapes-plugin` | Flag a symlink under a plugin that resolves outside the plugin but inside the marketplace. Condition: a marketplace install copies its target; a local-path install and a `command` copy-mode install skip it. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^plugins-host-marketplace-share-files-within-a-marketplace-with-symlinks] [^plugins-loading-in-place-and-copied-plugins] |
| `plugin-symlink-in-component-dir` | No symlinked `skills`, `agents` or `commands` directory, and no symlinked entry inside one. validate skips and warns (or errors when the named directory is a link). | recommended | warn | practice | covered | [^plugins-cli-reference-validate-a-directory] |
| `plugin-no-git-lfs` | No `.gitattributes` `filter=lfs` pattern matches a file under a plugin. Git installs never download LFS content, so files arrive as pointers. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugins-host-marketplace-keep-plugin-files-out-of-git-lfs] |
| `plugin-package-lockfile` | A plugin-root `package.json` has a supported lockfile (`bun.lock`, `bun.lockb`, `npm-shrinkwrap.json`, `package-lock.json`). Flag only `yarn.lock`/`pnpm-lock.yaml`, and `bunfig.toml` beside a Bun lockfile. The install is skipped. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-loading-when-the-dependency-install-runs] [^plugins-loading-when-the-dependency-install-fails-or-is-skipped] |
| `plugin-package-lockfile-choice` | At most one supported lockfile (first match wins, in the order above), and prefer an npm lockfile. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-loading-when-the-dependency-install-runs] |
| `plugin-npm-source-shrinkwrap` | A plugin whose marketplace entry uses an `npm` source ships `npm-shrinkwrap.json`; npm drops `package-lock.json` from packages (cross-file). | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-loading-when-the-dependency-install-runs] |
| `plugin-package-lifecycle-scripts` | Flag `preinstall`, `install`, `postinstall` in a plugin-root `package.json`. The install runs with `--ignore-scripts`. | recommended | warn | limit | – | [^plugins-loading-limits-on-the-dependency-install] |
| `plugin-relevance-schema` | Marketplace entry `relevance` is an object with only `topic` (string) and `signals` (object); `signals` keys only `cwd`, `cli`, `hosts`, `filesRead`, `manifestDeps`. Unknown keys and a non-object value are ignored. | recommended | error | no-op | covered | [^plugins-relevance-field-reference] [^plugins-relevance-validate-your-marketplace] |
| `plugin-relevance-limits` | `topic` max 64 chars; `cwd` max 10 x 256; `cli` max 10 x 64; `hosts` max 20 x 128; `filesRead` max 10 x 256; `manifestDeps` max 10, each value max 256. Over-limit invalidates the entry. | recommended | error | limit | covered | [^plugins-relevance-relevance] [^plugins-relevance-relevancesignals] [^plugins-relevance-field-reference] |
| `plugin-relevance-signals` | At least one signal set. `hosts` entries are bare lowercase hostnames (no scheme, port, path). `manifestDeps` entries have `file` and `pattern`, each a valid JavaScript `RegExp`. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-relevance-relevance] [^plugins-relevance-relevancesignals] [^plugins-relevance-manifest-dependency-matching] |
| `plugin-relevance-signal-hints` | (heuristic) `cli` entries are one command name with no spaces. `manifestDeps` `file` patterns end with `$`, not start with `^`. | off | warn | no-op | – | [^plugins-relevance-command-name-matching] [^plugins-relevance-manifest-dependency-matching] |
| `plugin-dir-not-marketplace-root` | (heuristic) A `--plugin-dir` or `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_DIRS` value in committed scripts names a plugin root or a folder of plugins, not a marketplace root whose plugins sit under `plugins/`. | off | warn | no-op | – | [^plugins-troubleshooting-plugin-dir-at-a-marketplace-root-doesnt-load-the-plugins-under-plugins] |

#### Eval suites (may be out of scope)

These rules lint `claude plugin eval` suites (`evals/` or the `experimental.evals` directory). Eval runs need early access and v2.1.269+. Drop this subsection if ADR 005 keeps evals out of scope.

| Rule | Checks | Preset | Severity | Category | Validate | Docs |
|------|--------|--------|----------|----------|----------|------|
| `plugin-evals-dir` | `experimental.evals` is a relative path of plain directory names: not absolute, no `..`. Else the run warns and uses `evals/`. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugin-evals-use-a-different-eval-directory] |
| `plugin-evals-prompt-frontmatter` | `prompt.md` keys only `schema_version`, `name`, `description`, `tags`, `plugins`, `runs`, `expected_outcome`, `model`, `max_turns`, `timeout_seconds`, `allowed_tools`, `append_system_prompt`, `env`. `runs` 1 to 50; `max_turns` max 200; `timeout_seconds` max 3600; `env` keys match `EVAL_[A-Z0-9_]*`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugin-evals-promptmd-frontmatter] |
| `plugin-evals-prompt-no-at-mentions` | No `@path` mentions in the `prompt.md` body; they are not expanded. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugin-evals-set-run-limits-and-tools-in-promptmd] |
| `plugin-evals-case-yaml` | `case.yaml` sets `schema_version: "1.1"` and `name`. `context.scaffold_script`, `context.history_file` and `context.add_dirs` exist inside the case directory. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugin-evals-caseyaml-fields] [^plugin-evals-seed-the-workspace-or-conversation] |
| `plugin-evals-grader` | Grader `type` in `regex`, `tool_used`, `tool_order`, `file_exists`, `llm`, `baseline`; `weight` positive; `arm` `with-only` or `both`; `target`/`focus` in `last_message`, `trace`, `files`, `{source: file, path}`, `mock_calls`; regex `pattern` has no inline `(?i)`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugin-evals-grader-frontmatter] [^plugin-evals-what-a-grader-can-look-at] [^plugin-evals-grader-types] |
| `plugin-evals-mock-schema` | Mock `type` is `fixed` or `agent`; `error` only with `fixed`; `abort_when` only with `agent`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugin-evals-mock-files] |
| `plugin-evals-mock-server-exists` | Each `mocks/<server>/` names a server in the plugin's MCP configuration (cross-file). | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugin-evals-mock-mcp-servers] |
| `plugin-evals-results-gitignored` | `evals/results/` is in `.gitignore`. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugin-evals-eval-suite-reference] |
| `plugin-evals-replay-committed` | `mocks/.replay/` is tracked, not ignored, so CI runs repeat. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugin-evals-replay-agent-mock-answers] |

#### Plugin manifest and layout: notes

- **`version` conflict.** `--strict` fails on a missing `version`, and the publish page says "Drop `--strict` if you chose to omit `version`" [^plugins-publish-prepare-your-plugin-for-release]. The hosting and loading pages recommend omitting `version` so users track commits [^plugins-host-marketplace-release-a-new-version] [^plugins-loading-how-claude-code-computes-the-version]. Decision 3 (see Decisions): the rule is `off` in `recommended` and `error` in `bje`. This repository sets `version` in all five `plugin.json` files and runs `claude plugin validate --strict` in `plugin-validate.yml`. `version` is not read for `command` sources, claude.ai-hosted marketplaces, or in-place local-directory loads [^plugins-manifest-reference-version].
- **Symlinks by install method** [^plugins-host-marketplace-share-files-within-a-marketplace-with-symlinks]. A marketplace install into the cache keeps in-plugin links as relative links. It dereferences links to elsewhere in the same marketplace (copies the target). It skips links outside the marketplace. A local-path install and a `command` source in `copy` mode keep only links that resolve inside the plugin directory, and skip all others. The docs say `--plugin-dir` loads the directory in place and never copies it [^plugins-loading-in-place-and-copied-plugins]. A retest on 2.1.284 confirms that `--plugin-dir` keeps a link that resolves outside the plugin (see Retest in the Method section). For component paths, the loading page allows "links between plugins within one marketplace" [^plugins-loading-paths-that-escape-the-plugin-directory]. validate does not follow links [^plugins-cli-reference-validate-a-directory].
- **bje preset.** Each `plugins/<p>/src/lib -> ../../../lib` link resolves inside the marketplace but outside the plugin. So `plugin-symlink-escapes-plugin` warns on every plugin here. The `bje` preset should turn it `off`, because this repo installs through the marketplace or `--plugin-dir`, and both keep the link. Candidate `bje` rules from CLAUDE.md, not added here because they have no docs source: no `package.json` in a plugin, and no bash files under `plugins/`. The Proposed `bje` preset section lists them.
- `plugin-settings-agent-exists`: the agents findings rated it "breaks" and the plugins findings "best-practice". The docs only say "Set `agent` to run one of the plugin's own agents" [^plugins-components-default-settings]. I used `warn` because they document no failure.
- `plugin-monitors-command-quote`: the hooks findings treat unquoted placeholders as "breaks". Failure needs an install path with spaces, so by policy this is a conditional `warn`. The hooks group should align `hooks-shell-form-quote-placeholder`. Both `"${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/x"` and `"${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}"/x` pass [^plugins-manifest-reference-quoting-and-path-separators].
- Changelog 2.1.136 says the `skills` key no longer hides the default `skills/`. 2.1.140 warns about default folders that a key replaces [^changelog-claude-code-changelog]. The manifest reference says `skills` adds to the default and does not replace it [^plugins-manifest-reference-how-each-key-combines-with-its-default-location]. So `plugin-default-dir-shadowed` excludes `skills`.
- `plugin-manifest-unknown-field`: changelog 2.1.281 accepts `privacyPolicyUrl`, `supportUrl` "and other listing metadata keys" without a full list [^changelog-claude-code-changelog]. The allowlist may need more keys.
- `plugin-path-containment` validate coverage is the `..` spelling. Before v2.1.257 even load-time checks looked only at spelling, not at symlink targets [^errors-path-escapes-plugin-directory].
- Overlap with other groups; dedupe at merge. `plugin-user-config-no-shell-fields` overlaps `hooks-user-config-exec-form-only` (hooks) and the `headersHelper` checks (mcp-lsp). `plugin-channels-schema` overlaps `channel-server-exists` (mcp-lsp). `plugin-path-uses-plugin-root` and `plugin-no-state-in-plugin-root` overlap `hooks-plugin-use-plugin-root` and `hooks-plugin-no-state-in-root`. `plugin-no-root-claude-md` sits between memory and plugin. `plugin-commands-dir-legacy` sits next to the skills group's legacy command rule. I kept each where the finding is about plugin layout or `userConfig`.
- Left to other groups: marketplace entry and cross-file `version` checks (entry vs `plugin.json`, set in both), entry name vs manifest name, `commands` paths in entries, `command` link-mode top-level symlinks (marketplace); plugin agent fields and output-style frontmatter (agents); hook script existence and exec bit (hooks); plugin `.mcp.json`/`.lsp.json` (mcp-lsp); plugin skill `description`, `name` prefix (skills).
- plugin-evals accepts a root `plugin.json` as well as `.claude-plugin/plugin.json` [^plugin-evals-requirements]. `plugin-manifest-location` should ignore a root `plugin.json` that only serves evals, or accept an option.
- Several footnote headings are HTML `<hN id>` headings whose `id` differs from the text slug. Examples: "Ask the user for configuration values" (`#user-configuration`), "Seed the workspace or conversation" (`#add-setup-or-history-with-case-yaml`), and the troubleshooting headings. The URL script must read the `id`. The errors heading text contains a literal `user\_config`.

### Marketplace manifest

Covers `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` (top level, entries, plugin sources, reserved names, cross-file checks against each entry's `plugin.json`), and the marketplace and plugin keys in committed settings files (`extraKnownMarketplaces`, `enabledPlugins`, the policy lists, and related keys). 57 rules. In the Validate column, `covered` means the docs say `claude plugin validate` reports every sub-check, `partial` means it reports some of them (Notes say which), and `–` means it reports none.

| Rule | Checks | Preset | Severity | Category | Validate | Docs |
|------|--------|--------|----------|----------|----------|------|
| `marketplace-location` | A marketplace file lives at `<root>/.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`. Report a `marketplace.json` elsewhere: `marketplace add` cannot find it, and users need `extraKnownMarketplaces` with `path`. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-marketplace-file] |
| `marketplace-schema` | Required `name`, `owner`, `plugins`. `owner` is an object with non-empty `name`, optional `email`, `url`. Types: `plugins` array; `description`, `version`, `$schema`, `metadata.description`, `metadata.version`, `metadata.pluginRoot` strings; `forceRemoveDeletedPlugins` boolean; `allowCrossMarketplaceDependenciesOn` string[]; `renames` object of string or `null`. Entry: `name`, `source` required; `category` string; `tags` string[]; `strict`, `defaultEnabled` boolean; `displayName` string; `headers` object; `headersHelper` string; `metadata`, `experimental`, `relevance` objects (else ignored). | recommended | error | load | partial | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-top-level-fields] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-plugin-entries] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `marketplace-unknown-field` | No key outside the documented set at the top level, under `metadata` (`description`, `version`, `pluginRoot`), or in an entry (entry fields plus every `plugin.json` field). Claude Code ignores unknown keys, so a typo loads silently. | recommended | error | no-op | covered | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-marketplace-file] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-description` | Top-level `description`, or `metadata.description`, is present. | recommended | warn | practice | covered | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-top-level-fields] |
| `marketplace-plugins-nonempty` | `plugins` has at least one entry. | recommended | warn | practice | covered | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-name-format` | `name` is non-empty; has no spaces, `/`, `\`, or `..`; is not `.`; has no control, invisible, or bidirectional-formatting characters; is ASCII only (non-ASCII counts as impersonation). | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-top-level-fields] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-reserved-names] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-name-reserved` | `name` is not an official, community, or directory name (19 names); not a spelling of one that differs only by a trailing dot or a non-underscore symbol for `-`; not `inline`, `builtin`, `skills-dir`, `synced`, `claude-plugin-test`; not `npm`, `pip`, `uv`, `cargo`, `github`, `gh` in any casing; does not start with `claudeai-`. Option `allowOfficial` for repos under `github.com/anthropics/`. | recommended | error | load | partial | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-reserved-names] [^errors-marketplace-is-registered-from-an-untrusted-source] [^errors-marketplace-name-is-another-spelling-of-a-reserved-name] [^plugins-create-marketplace-problems-that-surface-when-you-add-or-install] |
| `marketplace-name-impersonation` | (heuristic) `name` does not imitate an official marketplace, such as `claude` or `anthropic` combined with `official`, `plugins`, or a version suffix (`official-claude-plugins`, `claude-plugins-v2`, `claude-official`). | off | warn | load | covered | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-reserved-names] [^errors-claude-code-refuses-the-marketplace-name] [^plugins-create-marketplace-problems-that-validation-reports] |
| `marketplace-name-claude-desktop` | Marketplace `name` is not `org`, `org-provisioned`, or `unknown` in any casing. Marketplace `name` and each `plugins[].name` match `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$`. Fails only in Claude Desktop managed sync. | recommended | warn | portability | covered | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] [^plugins-troubleshooting-marketplace-validation-errors] |
| `marketplace-entry-name-format` | Each `plugins[].name` has no spaces and no control or bidirectional-formatting characters. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-plugin-entries] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-entry-name-unique` | No two `plugins[]` entries share a `name`. | recommended | error | consistency | covered | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-entry-name-matches-manifest` | (cross-file) For a relative-path entry, entry `name` equals the `name` in the source's `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`. | recommended | error | consistency | – | [^plugins-create-marketplace-keep-the-entry-name-and-the-manifest-name-the-same] [^plugins-loading-entry-name-and-manifest-name] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `marketplace-self-hosted-root-source` | (cross-file) A `marketplace.json` beside a `plugin.json` in one `.claude-plugin/` has an entry with `source` `"./"` whose `name` equals the `plugin.json` `name`. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-publish-add-the-marketplace-file-to-your-repository] |
| `marketplace-relative-source-format` | A string `source` starts with `./`, or is `"."`, or is a bare name (no `/`) when `metadata.pluginRoot` is set. It is not absolute, has no `..`, and is not network-shaped (UNC, `//`). `metadata.pluginRoot` is relative and inside the marketplace. | recommended | error | load | partial | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-plugin-sources] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-relative-path-plugin-source] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-bare-names-under-pluginroot] [^errors-marketplace-entry-path-does-not-stay-inside-the-marketplace-directory] |
| `marketplace-relative-source-backslash` | A string `source` has no `\` after the leading `./`. Fails on macOS and Linux only. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-relative-path-plugin-source] [^errors-marketplace-entry-path-does-not-stay-inside-the-marketplace-directory] |
| `marketplace-relative-source-exists` | (cross-file) A relative `source`, resolved from the marketplace root (the directory that holds `.claude-plugin/`), names an existing directory. Validate passes; install fails with `Source path does not exist`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugins-create-marketplace-write-relative-paths-from-the-marketplace-root] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-marketplace-file] |
| `marketplace-relative-source-escape-symlink` | (cross-file) No symlink on a relative `source` path resolves outside the marketplace root. | recommended | error | security | – | [^errors-marketplace-entry-path-does-not-stay-inside-the-marketplace-directory] |
| `marketplace-relative-source-symlink` | (cross-file) A relative `source` is not, and does not traverse, a symlink inside the marketplace. Validate then skips that plugin's `plugin.json`. | recommended | warn | limit | covered | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-relative-source-url-hosted` | (heuristic) When the repo documents or registers the marketplace as a direct `marketplace.json` URL (`url` source), no entry uses a relative `source`. | off | warn | load | – | [^plugins-host-marketplace-avoid-relative-path-entries-in-a-url-hosted-marketplace] [^plugins-troubleshooting-plugins-with-relative-paths-fail-in-url-based-marketplaces] |
| `marketplace-source-schema` | Object `source.source` is `github`, `url`, `git-subdir`, `npm`, `archive`, or `command` (not `git`, not `unsupported`). `github`: `repo` `owner/repo`, no `path`. `url`: `url` with `https://`, `http://`, `file://`, or `git@`. `git-subdir`: `url` and `path`. `npm`: `package` without `..`. `sha`: `^[0-9a-f]{40}$`. `archive`: `https://` URL, not loopback, link-local, or metadata host; `sha256` `^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$`. `command`: printable ASCII, `max` 500 chars, no run of 4+ spaces; `timeout` integer 1 to 600; `mode` `copy` or `link`. | recommended | error | load | partial | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-plugin-sources] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-invalid-input-on-a-source] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-url-plugin-source] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-git-subdir-plugin-source] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-archive-plugin-source] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-command-plugin-source] |
| `marketplace-archive-sha256` | An `archive` source sets `sha256`, so Claude Code refuses a changed download. | recommended | warn | security | partial | [^plugins-host-marketplace-serve-users-who-have-no-git-host-account] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-command-source` | Report each `command` source for review: it runs a shell command on the user's machine at install, update, and once per session. | recommended | warn | security | – | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-command-plugin-source] |
| `marketplace-command-link-mode` | A `command` source with `mode: "link"` cannot install on Windows. Fails on Windows only. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-copy-mode-and-link-mode] |
| `marketplace-command-version-ignored` | An entry with a `command` source sets no `version`. Claude Code ignores it for command sources. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-loading-how-claude-code-computes-the-version] |
| `marketplace-version-duplicate` | (cross-file) `version` is set in at most one of the entry and the source's `plugin.json`. `plugin.json` wins without warning; validate warns only on a mismatch for relative-path entries. | recommended | error | no-op | partial | [^plugins-host-marketplace-release-a-new-version] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-headers-archive-only` | Entry `headers` and `headersHelper` appear only on an entry whose `source` is `archive`. | recommended | error | no-op | covered | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-headers-helper-strict` | An entry that sets `headersHelper` also sets `"strict": false`. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-plugin-entries] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-headers-routing` | Entry `headers` has no request-routing or identity names: `Host`, `Cookie`, `X-Forwarded-*`. Claude Code drops them. | recommended | error | no-op | covered | [^plugins-host-marketplace-when-claude-code-skips-a-headershelper-command-or-drops-its-output] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-headers-helper-command` | Entry and `url`-source `headersHelper`: printable ASCII, `max` 500 chars, no run of 4+ spaces. The first word is not a relative path (`./`, `../`), which resolves against `~/.claude`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugins-host-marketplace-write-the-headershelper-command] |
| `marketplace-headers-literal-credential` | (heuristic) No literal credential (for example `Bearer <token>`) in entry `headers` or in an `extraKnownMarketplaces` `url` source's `headers`. Use `${VAR}` or `headersHelper`. | off | warn | security | – | [^plugins-host-marketplace-authenticate-archive-downloads] [^settings-reference-marketplace-source-types] |
| `marketplace-entry-hooks-inline` | Entry `hooks` is an inline object of event names to matcher arrays, not a path string or array. Validate passes; the hooks never run. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-hooks-in-an-entry] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-failures-that-validation-doesnt-catch] |
| `marketplace-strict-false-conflict` | (cross-file) An entry with `"strict": false` whose source has a `plugin.json` declares none of `commands`, `agents`, `skills`, `hooks`, `outputStyles`, `themes`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-strict-mode] [^plugins-troubleshooting-plugin-name-has-conflicting-manifests] |
| `marketplace-entry-manifest-only-fields` | (cross-file) An entry whose source has a `plugin.json` sets none of `mcpServers`, `lspServers`, `userConfig`, `channels`. They do not apply. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-entry-and-plugin-json] |
| `marketplace-entry-hooks-override` | (cross-file) With `strict` unset or `true`, entry `hooks` and `plugin.json` hooks do not both declare the same event. The entry's matchers replace the manifest's for that event. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-how-entry-fields-combine-with-pluginjson] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-strict-mode] |
| `marketplace-entry-root-skills` | (cross-file) An entry whose `source` is the marketplace root and that lists `skills` names every skill directory under the default `skills/`. Unlisted ones do not load. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-manifest-reference-how-entry-fields-combine-with-pluginjson] |
| `marketplace-entry-component-paths` | (cross-file) Entry component paths (`commands`, `agents`, `skills`, `outputStyles`, `themes`) follow the plugin path rules: start with `./`, no `..`, no backslash, exist, and resolve inside the entry's plugin directory. | recommended | error | load | – | [^errors-path-escapes-plugin-directory] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `marketplace-renames-resolve` | Each `renames` chain ends at a name in `plugins[]` or at `null`, has no cycle, and each target is a valid plugin name. | recommended | error | load | covered | [^plugins-host-marketplace-migrate-users-with-a-renames-map] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages] |
| `marketplace-renames-history` | (cross-file, needs git base ref) Existing `renames` entries are not edited or deleted. An entry name removed from `plugins[]` gains a `renames` key (new name or `null`). | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^plugins-host-marketplace-migrate-users-with-a-renames-map] |
| `marketplace-min-version` | Reports a feature above option `minVersion` (unset: rule inactive): `renames` v2.1.193, entry `metadata` v2.1.222, `archive` v2.1.224, `command` v2.1.229, `headers`/`headersHelper` v2.1.238, `metadata.pluginRoot` v2.1.239. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^plugins-marketplace-reference-top-level-fields] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-plugin-entries] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-plugin-sources] |
| `settings-enabled-plugins-schema` | `enabledPlugins` keys are `plugin-name@marketplace-name` (one `@`, both parts non-empty); values are Boolean. | recommended | error | load | – | [^settings-reference-enabledplugins] [^plugins-org-require-a-marketplace-and-its-plugins] |
| `settings-enabled-plugins-entry-exists` | (cross-file) When the `@marketplace` part names a marketplace whose `marketplace.json` is in the repo, the plugin part equals an entry `name` in it, not a `plugin.json` name. | recommended | error | consistency | – | [^plugins-loading-entry-name-and-manifest-name] [^plugins-manifest-reference-metadata-precedence] |
| `settings-enabled-plugins-marketplace-declared` | (heuristic) Each `@marketplace` part in a committed `enabledPlugins` is declared in `extraKnownMarketplaces`, or is `claude-plugins-official`, `inline`, `skills-dir`, or `synced`. | off | warn | consistency | – | [^settings-reference-enabledplugins] [^plugins-org-require-a-marketplace-and-its-plugins] [^plugins-loading-find-where-a-plugin-came-from] |
| `settings-enabled-plugins-external-source` | (cross-file) A plugin set `true` in `.claude/settings.json` whose repo-local marketplace entry has a non-relative `source` is not installed for teammates. | recommended | warn | limit | – | [^plugins-loading-enabled-in-project-settings-but-not-installed] [^plugins-org-require-plugins-per-repository] |
| `settings-extra-known-marketplaces-schema` | Each value is `{source, autoUpdate?}` (Boolean). `source.source` is `github` (`repo`, one repo, no `*`), `git` (`url`), `url` (`url`), `file` (`path` ending `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`), `directory` (`path`), or `settings` (`name` equal to key, not reserved; `plugins` items with object `source` and fields `name`, `source`, `description`, `version`, `strict`, `headers`, `headersHelper`). Not `npm`, `skills-dir`, `hostPattern`, `pathPattern`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^settings-reference-extraknownmarketplaces] [^settings-reference-marketplace-source-types] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-marketplace-sources] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-fields-by-type] [^settings-reference-owner-wildcards] |
| `settings-extra-known-marketplaces-key-matches-name` | (cross-file) Each `extraKnownMarketplaces` key equals the `name` in the `marketplace.json` it points at, when that file is in the repo. | recommended | error | consistency | – | [^plugins-org-require-a-marketplace-and-its-plugins] |
| `settings-extra-known-marketplaces-directory` | A committed `.claude/settings.json` does not use a `directory` marketplace source, which is for development only. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^settings-reference-marketplace-source-types] |
| `settings-marketplace-skip-lfs` | No `skipLfs` in a marketplace `source`. It is accepted and has no effect since v2.1.274. | recommended | warn | deprecated | – | [^settings-reference-marketplace-source-types] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-fields-by-type] |
| `settings-marketplace-headers-helper-https` | An `extraKnownMarketplaces` `url` source with `headersHelper` has a `url` that starts with `https://`. Otherwise the command never runs. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^plugins-host-marketplace-when-claude-code-skips-a-headershelper-command-or-drops-its-output] |
| `settings-marketplace-key-alias-conflict` | A settings file does not set both `extraKnownMarketplaces` and `additionalMarketplaces`, or both `strictKnownMarketplaces` and `allowedMarketplaces`. The alias is ignored. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^settings-reference-marketplace-key-aliases] [^plugins-org-aliases-for-the-marketplace-keys] |
| `settings-marketplace-key-alias` | Use `extraKnownMarketplaces` and `strictKnownMarketplaces`, not the aliases `additionalMarketplaces` and `allowedMarketplaces`. Clients before v2.1.232 ignore the aliases. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^settings-reference-marketplace-key-aliases] [^plugins-org-aliases-for-the-marketplace-keys] |

| `settings-known-marketplaces-policy-schema` | In managed settings files, each `strictKnownMarketplaces` and `blockedMarketplaces` entry is a source object: `github` (`repo`), `git` (`url`), `url` (`url`), `file` and `directory` (absolute `path`), `hostPattern`, `pathPattern` (valid regex), or `skills-dir`. A `repo` wildcard is only `owner/*`. No `npm` entry (matches nothing). `pluginTrustMessage` is a string. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^settings-reference-allowed-source-types] [^settings-reference-owner-wildcards] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-marketplace-sources] [^plugins-org-allowlist-with-strictknownmarketplaces] [^settings-reference-plugintrustmessage] |

#### Marketplace manifest: notes
- Validate `partial`: `marketplace-schema` (validate reports missing required fields, empty `owner.name`, and non-object `relevance`/`metadata`/`experimental`, but the docs do not state field type checks); `marketplace-name-reserved` (validate reports internal and package names only; exact official names, spelling variants, and `claudeai-` pass validate and fail at add); `marketplace-relative-source-format` (validate reports a missing `./` and `..`, not absolute or network-shaped paths); `marketplace-source-schema` (`Invalid input` covers unknown type, missing field, npm `..`; SHA, host, and command limits are not stated); `marketplace-archive-sha256` (validate warns only when `headersHelper` is set); `marketplace-version-duplicate` (mismatch only, relative-path entries only). Validate never reads project `.claude/settings*.json`, so every `settings-*` rule is `–`. [^plugins-create-marketplace-problems-that-validation-reports] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-validation-messages]
- Impersonation: validate reports look-alike names (`claude-official`), but exact official names and spelling variants pass it. So `marketplace-name-impersonation` is `covered` but stays `off` as a heuristic, and `marketplace-name-reserved` does the exact list. [^plugins-create-marketplace-problems-that-validation-reports] [^plugins-marketplace-reference-reserved-names]
- `marketplace-location`: the Phase 1 row called this "breaks". The docs give a working path (`extraKnownMarketplaces` with `path`), so it is `practice`, warn. [^plugins-marketplace-reference-marketplace-file]
- `marketplace-version-duplicate`: Phase 1 had two rows (both set; both set and different). One implementation handles both; the stricter documented rule ("Don't set `version` in both") wins, so it reports equal values too. [^plugins-host-marketplace-release-a-new-version]
- `marketplace-entry-name-matches-manifest`: the changelog row said "degraded" (Enable/Disable bug, fixed v2.1.195). The create-marketplace page says install by manifest name fails. Chose the stricter: error. [^plugins-create-marketplace-keep-the-entry-name-and-the-manifest-name-the-same] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog]
- Backslash in a relative `source`: Phase 1 rated it "breaks". It fails only on macOS and Linux, so per policy it is recommended warn (`portability`). [^plugins-marketplace-reference-relative-path-plugin-source]
- `settings-marketplace-skip-lfs`: the field has no effect, which reads as `no-op`. It was a real option before v2.1.274, and its old intent (skip LFS) is now the default, so it is `deprecated`, warn. [^settings-reference-marketplace-source-types]
- `settings-strict-known-marketplaces-skills-dir`: Phase 1 rated it "silently-ignored". Leaving out `skills-dir` can be a deliberate policy, so it is `practice`, warn. [^plugins-org-keep-skills-directory-plugins-loading]
- `marketplace-command-source` and `marketplace-archive-sha256` are security rows that only weaken a default, so warn. `marketplace-relative-source-escape-symlink` is `security` category because the docs refuse such links "for security", but its impact is a failed install, so error.
- Added from the docs during spot-checks, with no Phase 1 row: `marketplace-command-link-mode`, `settings-marketplace-headers-helper-https`, `settings-plugin-suggestion-marketplaces-source`, `settings-enabled-plugins-entry-exists` (derived from the entry-name vs manifest-name rule), and the relative-`headersHelper` sub-check. [^plugins-marketplace-reference-copy-mode-and-link-mode] [^plugins-host-marketplace-write-the-headershelper-command] [^settings-reference-pluginsuggestionmarketplaces]
- `settings-enabled-plugins-marketplace-declared` must exempt `claude-plugins-official`: an `enabledPlugins` `true` for it declares the marketplace by itself. [^plugins-org-require-a-marketplace-and-its-plugins]
- `marketplace-renames-history` needs a git base ref (diff against the merge base). An ESLint rule cannot see history on its own; ship it with a `baseRef` option or as a CI script.
- Handed to other groups (ownership): `relevance` limits and shape (`plugin`), `dependencies` shape, resolution, and `allowCrossMarketplaceDependenciesOn` membership (`plugin`, reads `marketplace.json`), `plugin.json` `name` kebab-case and rename history (`plugin`), `allowedChannelPlugins` and `channelsEnabled` (`mcp-lsp`), `strictPluginOnlyCustomization` (`settings`), `pluginConfigs["agents-md@builtin"]` (`memory`), `skillOverrides` on plugin skills (`skills`), `.claude/skills/*/.claude-plugin/plugin.json` layout (`plugin`). The `settings` group should drop `syncClaudeAiPlugins` from its scope rule, since `settings-sync-claude-ai-plugins` owns it here.
- Open question: `defaultEnabled` set differently in an entry and its `plugin.json` is a silent override (entry wins). No Phase 1 row; a future `marketplace-default-enabled-conflict` would be cross-file, error. [^plugins-manifest-reference-metadata-precedence]

### CLAUDE.md, rules and memory

Rules for instruction files: `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/CLAUDE.md`, `CLAUDE.local.md`, `AGENTS.md` and its variants, `@path` imports, `.claude/rules/**/*.md`, subagent `agent-memory` directories, the gitignore state of local files, and the settings keys that control instruction loading. 39 rules: 13 error, 10 warn in `recommended`, 16 heuristic rules `off`.

| Rule | Checks | Preset | Severity | Category | Validate | Docs |
|------|--------|--------|----------|----------|----------|------|
| `claude-md-max-bytes` | `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/CLAUDE.md` and `CLAUDE.local.md` are at most `max` 4,194,304 bytes (4 MiB). Claude Code skips a larger file entirely. | recommended | error | limit | – | [^memory-how-it-works] [^memory-my-claude-md-is-too-large] |
| `claude-md-import-exists` | Each `@path` token outside code spans and fences resolves to an existing file. Resolve relative paths against the importing file's directory, not the cwd. Skip `~/` and paths outside the repo. Targets: CLAUDE.md files, `AGENTS.md`, imported files. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^memory-import-additional-files] [^memory-write-effective-instructions] |
| `claude-md-import-max-depth` | A recursive `@path` import chain from any instruction file is at most `max` 4 hops. Imports past that depth do not load. (cross-file) | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^memory-import-additional-files] |
| `claude-md-agents-md-shadowed` | An `AGENTS.md` or `.claude/AGENTS.md` is not read by default when a `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/CLAUDE.md` or `CLAUDE.local.md` exists in its directory or an ancestor. Pass only if such a CLAUDE.md imports `@AGENTS.md` or is a symlink to it. (cross-file) | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^memory-when-claude-code-reads-agents-md] [^memory-share-one-file-with-other-coding-tools] |
| `claude-md-agents-md-variant` | Flag `AGENTS.local.md`, `AGENTS.override.md` and any file under a `.agents/` directory. Claude Code never reads them. Option `allow` lists paths kept on purpose for other tools. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^memory-when-claude-code-reads-agents-md] |
| `memory-symlink-network-target` | A `CLAUDE.md` or `.claude/rules/` symlink (file or directory) does not target a UNC share (`\\server\share`), `/net/...` or `/Network/...`. `\\wsl$` targets are allowed. Claude Code does not follow such a link. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^memory-share-rules-across-projects-with-symlinks] |
| `rules-symlink-external-scoped` | A rule reached through a symlink whose target is outside the repo root has no `paths` frontmatter. After external-import approval, only unscoped external rules load, so a path-scoped one never loads. (cross-file) | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^memory-share-rules-across-projects-with-symlinks] |
| `rules-md-extension` | Every file under `.claude/rules/` (recursive) has the `.md` extension. Only `.md` files are discovered; others are ignored. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^memory-set-up-rules] |
| `rules-frontmatter-schema` | Rule frontmatter: the opening `---` is line 1; the YAML parses (unquoted globs that start with `*` fail); `paths` is the only key; `paths` is a list of strings or one comma-separated string. A failure drops the `paths` scope or the key. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^memory-rule-frontmatter-reference] [^glossary-frontmatter] [^claude-directory-frontmatter-fields-by-file] |
| `rules-paths-glob-valid` | Each `paths` glob is valid: an unescaped `[` forms a bracket expression (else escape as `\[`). The brace expansion of the whole list stays within `maxExpanded` 1,000 patterns and `maxBytes` 4 MiB. A failing pattern matches nothing. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^memory-path-specific-rules] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `memory-settings-schema` | In any settings file: `autoMemoryEnabled` is a boolean; `autoMemoryDirectory` is a string that is absolute or starts with `~/`; `claudeMdExcludes` is an array of strings; `pluginConfigs["agents-md@builtin"].options.instructionFiles` is `claude-md-or-agents-md`, `claude-md-and-agents-md`, `claude-md` or `managed-only`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^settings-reference-automemorydirectory] [^settings-reference-automemoryenabled] [^settings-reference-claudemdexcludes] [^memory-choose-which-instruction-files-load] |
| `claude-md-excludes-pattern` | Each `claudeMdExcludes` entry starts with `/`, `**/` or a Windows drive. Patterns match absolute paths, so a relative-style pattern such as `packages/web/**` never matches. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^large-codebases-exclude-irrelevant-claude-md-files] [^memory-exclude-specific-claude-md-files] |
| `claude-md-max-lines` | `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/CLAUDE.md`, `CLAUDE.local.md`, `AGENTS.md` and each `@path`-imported file have at most `max` 200 lines. Over the recommended length, Claude Code shows a startup and `/status` warning. | recommended | warn | limit | – | [^memory-write-effective-instructions] [^memory-my-claude-md-is-too-large] [^features-overview-compare-similar-features] [^costs-move-instructions-from-claude-md-to-skills] |
| `rules-max-lines` | Each `.claude/rules/**/*.md` file has at most `max` 200 lines. Each rules file counts as a separate file for the length warning. | recommended | warn | limit | – | [^memory-my-claude-md-is-too-large] |
| `memory-index-max-size` | A committed `.claude/agent-memory/*/MEMORY.md` has at most `maxLines` 200 lines and `maxBytes` 25,000 bytes, measured after YAML frontmatter and block HTML comments are removed. Content past either limit does not load. | recommended | warn | limit | – | [^sub-agents-enable-persistent-memory] [^memory-how-it-works] [^errors-memory-index-is-over-its-read-limit] |
| `claude-md-import-external` | In committed instruction files (`CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/CLAUDE.md`, `AGENTS.md`, imported files), no `@path` resolves outside the repo root (absolute, `~/`, or `../` escape). It needs per-user approval; a decline disables it for good. `AGENTS.md` never prompts. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^memory-import-additional-files] [^memory-where-agents-md-differs-from-claude-md] |
| `rules-symlink-external` | No `.claude/rules/` entry, and no `.claude` directory, is a symlink whose target is outside the repo root. Such rules load only after external-import approval. (cross-file) | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^memory-share-rules-across-projects-with-symlinks] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `claude-md-symlink` | A committed `CLAUDE.md` is a regular file, not a git symlink (mode 120000). Windows clones without `core.symlinks` get a one-line file. Edit and Write refuse to write through a symlink. Prefer `@AGENTS.md`. Fails on Windows only. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^memory-share-one-file-with-other-coding-tools] |
| `claude-md-local-untracked` | `CLAUDE.local.md` is not tracked by git, and the repo `.gitignore` has a pattern that covers it. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^memory-import-additional-files] [^claude-directory-whats-not-shown] |
| `memory-agent-memory-local-untracked` | No file under `.claude/agent-memory-local/` is tracked by git. `memory: local` subagent memory must stay out of version control. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^sub-agents-enable-persistent-memory] |
| `memory-auto-memory-directory-committed` | `.claude/settings.json` and `.claude/settings.local.json` do not set `autoMemoryDirectory`. A repo-supplied value is trust-gated, and is ignored while `permissions.blockReadsOutsideWorkingDirectories` is on. | recommended | warn | security | – | [^memory-storage-location] [^settings-reference-automemorydirectory] |
| `claude-md-combined-size` | The sum of lines in the always-loaded set (root and ancestor CLAUDE.md files, `CLAUDE.local.md`, unscoped rules, expanded `@path` imports) is at most `max`. No default: the combined limit is not documented. (heuristic, cross-file) | off | warn | limit | – | [^memory-my-claude-md-is-too-large] [^memory-set-up-rules] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `claude-md-import-in-code-span` | Flag an `@path` inside a code span or fence that resolves to an existing file. Import parsing skips code, so an intended import there does not load. (heuristic) | off | warn | no-op | – | [^memory-import-additional-files] |
| `claude-md-html-comment-content` | Flag instruction-like text (imperatives, `MUST`, `@path`) inside a block-level HTML comment outside code. Claude Code strips these comments before injection. (heuristic) | off | warn | no-op | – | [^memory-how-claude-md-files-load] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `claude-md-agents-md-prose-pointer` | Flag a CLAUDE.md that tells Claude in words to read `AGENTS.md` without an `@AGENTS.md` import. Use the import, or delete the CLAUDE.md. (heuristic) | off | warn | practice | – | [^memory-remove-an-earlier-agents-md-workaround] |
| `claude-md-location` | Flag instruction-like files at names or places Claude Code does not load, such as `.claude/CLAUDE.local.md` or a case variant like `claude.md`. Recognized: `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/CLAUDE.md`, `CLAUDE.local.md` per directory. (heuristic) | off | warn | no-op | – | [^memory-choose-where-to-put-claude-md-files] [^memory-load-from-additional-directories] |
| `rules-paths-no-match` | Each `paths` glob matches at least one tracked file. `*.md` matches root files only. (heuristic: a glob can target files that do not exist yet) | off | warn | practice | – | [^memory-path-specific-rules] |
| `claude-md-excludes-absolute-committed` | `claudeMdExcludes` in committed `.claude/settings.json` has no machine-specific absolute path. Put personal exclusions in `.claude/settings.local.json`. (heuristic) | off | warn | portability | – | [^large-codebases-exclude-irrelevant-claude-md-files] [^memory-exclude-specific-claude-md-files] |
| `memory-agent-memory-orphan` | Each `.claude/agent-memory/<name>/` matches a project subagent whose `name` is `<name>` and that sets `memory: project`. User and plugin agents can also own it. (heuristic, cross-file) | off | warn | consistency | – | [^sub-agents-enable-persistent-memory] |
| `memory-index-entry-format` | Each entry in a `MEMORY.md` index is one line; detail goes in topic files. (heuristic) | off | warn | practice | – | [^memory-how-it-works] |
| `memory-topic-frontmatter` | In agent-memory topic files with frontmatter, `type` is `user`, `feedback`, `project` or `reference`, and `modified` is an ISO 8601 timestamp. Documented for main auto memory only. (heuristic) | off | warn | consistency | – | [^memory-auto-memory] [^memory-how-it-works] |
| `claude-md-git-instructions` | If CLAUDE.md or a rule sets commit or pull request rules, settings set `includeGitInstructions: false`. (heuristic, cross-file) | off | warn | practice | – | [^memory-claude-isnt-following-my-claude-md] |
| `claude-md-emphasis-overuse` | Lines with `IMPORTANT`, `MUST` or all-caps emphasis are at most `max`. No default is documented. (heuristic) | off | warn | practice | – | [^best-practices-write-an-effective-claude-md] |
| `claude-md-derivable-content` | Flag content Claude can derive from the code: fenced directory trees, dependency lists, file-by-file descriptions. (heuristic) | off | warn | practice | – | [^memory-my-claude-md-is-too-large] |
| `claude-md-procedure-to-skill` | Flag a numbered procedure longer than `maxSteps` in CLAUDE.md; move it to a skill or a path-scoped rule. No default is documented. (heuristic) | off | warn | practice | – | [^memory-when-to-add-to-claude-md] |
| `claude-md-guardrail-to-hook` | Flag guardrail wording ("never edit X", "always run Y before commit") in CLAUDE.md or rules. Enforce it with a `PreToolUse` hook or permissions. (heuristic) | off | warn | practice | – | [^features-overview-compare-similar-features] [^memory-claude-isnt-following-my-claude-md] |
| `claude-md-dangling-reference` | Backticked repo paths and `/name` commands in instruction prose exist in the repo. (heuristic) | off | warn | consistency | – | [^memory-write-effective-instructions] |

#### CLAUDE.md, rules and memory: notes

- 200-line target vs scaling threshold: memory.md gives a 200-line target and a warning at the "recommended length". The changelog says the "too long" threshold "now scales with the model's context window". Per the caller, `claude-md-max-lines` and `rules-max-lines` use 200 as a documented target (warn, option `max`). [^memory-my-claude-md-is-too-large] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog]
- 4 MiB is a hard skip, so `claude-md-max-bytes` is an error. memory.md names only CLAUDE.md files for it. Whether it applies to `AGENTS.md` is not documented, so the rule leaves `AGENTS.md` out. [^memory-how-it-works]
- "25KB" is not defined as 25,000 or 25,600 bytes. `memory-index-max-size` uses the stricter 25,000. Only content that loads counts. Partial truncation is "degraded", so the rule is warn, not error. [^errors-memory-index-is-over-its-read-limit]
- External rule symlinks conflict: memory.md says the approval prompt appears "only when a project memory file imports a file outside the working directory with `@path`, not for symlinks alone". The 2.1.284 changelog says a fix now shows the prompt for rules and for a symlinked `.claude` directory. Both agree that approval is needed, so the rules assume it. The path-scoped case never loads, so it is a separate error rule. [^memory-share-rules-across-projects-with-symlinks] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog]
- `claude-md-agents-md-shadowed` is an error because the default (`claude-md-or-agents-md`) ignores `AGENTS.md`. A user can change it only in user or managed settings. A linter sees ancestors only up to the repo root, and sees `CLAUDE.local.md` only where it exists on disk. [^memory-when-claude-code-reads-agents-md]
- `claude-md-agents-md-variant` follows the error policy, but a repo can keep `AGENTS.override.md` or `.agents/` for other tools on purpose. Hence the `allow` option. [^memory-when-claude-code-reads-agents-md]
- `claude-md-import-exists`: the docs do not define token boundaries for `@path`. The rule treats `@` at the start of a word, outside code, as an import. Emails do not match. Tokens such as `@scope/pkg` that are not meant as imports need the option `ignorePattern`. [^memory-import-additional-files]
- `claude-md-import-external` skips `CLAUDE.local.md`. memory.md suggests `@~/.claude/my-project-instructions.md` for personal imports but does not say which file holds it. A committed file with that import would prompt every teammate. [^memory-import-additional-files]
- `rules-frontmatter-schema` cites the "Rule frontmatter reference" heading. In memory.md that heading is an HTML `<h4 id="rules-frontmatter-reference">`, not a `#` heading. If the footnote checker reads only `#` headings, use "Path-specific rules" instead. [^memory-rule-frontmatter-reference]
- Both `./CLAUDE.md` and `./.claude/CLAUDE.md`: no page says whether both load or one wins. The case sensitivity of `CLAUDE.md` is not documented either, so `claude-md-location` stays a heuristic. [^memory-set-up-a-project-claude-md]
- `memory-settings-scope` overlaps with the settings group's generic scope-only-key rule (`claudeMd` is "Managed", `pluginConfigs` is "User or managed"). Keep one implementation. [^settings-reference-claudemd] [^settings-reference-pluginconfigs]
- `claude plugin validate` does not check any file in this group. It warns only on a plugin-root `CLAUDE.md`, which the plugin group owns. `/doctor` and `/doctor prompt-audit` cover `claude-md-derivable-content`, `claude-md-import-exists` and `claude-md-dangling-reference` at runtime, not in CI. [^memory-write-effective-instructions]
- Left to other groups (seen in phase 1 rows): plugin-root `CLAUDE.md` (plugin); a `SessionStart` hook that prints `AGENTS.md`, `InstructionsLoaded` matcher values (hooks); `memory` enum, `omitClaudeMd`, and `memory` with `autoMemoryEnabled: false` (agents); `worktree.sparsePaths` without `.claude`, `.worktreeinclude` location, and `CLAUDE_CODE_ADDITIONAL_DIRECTORIES_CLAUDE_MD` with `additionalDirectories` (settings, permissions); `paths` in `SKILL.md` (skills).
- Legacy `.claude/` entries: the only ones the docs name (`todos/`, `statsig/`, `logs/`, `image-cache/`) are under `~/.claude`, not in a repo. No rule.

### Settings

Covers `.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.local.json`, committed `managed-settings.json` and `managed-settings.d/*.json`, files passed to `--settings`, the `env` block, model and output-style selection keys, statusline config, and `keybindings.json`: 63 rules. Keys owned by the permissions, sandbox, hooks, MCP, marketplace, and memory groups are checked there, except for scope (`settings-key-scope`) and unknown-key detection (`settings-schema`).

| Rule | Checks | Preset | Severity | Category | Validate | Docs |
|------|--------|--------|----------|----------|----------|------|
| `settings-valid-json` | File parses as strict JSON (no `//` comments, no trailing commas) with a top-level object. A failure rejects the whole user, project, or local file (Settings Error). | recommended | error | load | – | [^settings-edit-a-settings-file] [^settings-fix-a-broken-settings-file] [^settings-example-your-own-settings] [^managed-settings-keys-that-fail-closed] |
| `settings-file-size` | Settings file, and any file a repo script passes to `--settings`, is a regular file of at most 2 MiB (`max` 2097152 bytes). | recommended | error | limit | – | [^errors-settings-file-exceeds-the-2mib-limit] [^cli-reference-cli-flags] |
| `settings-schema-url` | `$schema` is present and equals `https://json.schemastore.org/claude-code-settings.json`. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^settings-edit-a-settings-file] |
| `settings-schema` | Unknown keys, top level and inside documented objects, against the plugin's own key catalog (Appendix A.1). Types, enums, ranges, and shapes per Appendix A.5. Env-var names used as top-level keys (`DISABLE_TELEMETRY`, `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER`, ...) belong in `env`. | recommended | error | load | – | [^settings-reference-settings-index] [^settings-fix-a-broken-settings-file] [^settings-reference-privacy-and-telemetry] [^settings-reference-autoupdateschannel] |
| `settings-key-scope` | Key whose Scope is `Managed`, `User or managed`, or `Global config` in `.claude/settings.json` or `.claude/settings.local.json`; `User, local, or managed` key in `.claude/settings.json`; `Global config` key in any settings file. Lists and value exceptions: Appendix A.1. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^settings-a-committed-key-doesnt-reach-teammates] [^settings-reference-settings-index] [^managed-settings-keys-only-a-managed-source-can-set] [^settings-reference-global-config-settings] |
| `settings-project-value-ignored` | In project or local files: `remoteControlAtStartup: true`, `isolatePeerMachines: false`, `disableClaudeAiConnectors: false`, `crossSessionInbound: "accept"`, `spinnerTipsOverride` tip objects/`tipsFile`/`label`/`excludeDefault`, `remote.defaultEnvironmentId` `ccpool_*`, `forceLoginMethod: "gateway"`, `forceLoginOrgUUID`, `bashEditDiffEnabled: true`. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^settings-exceptions-to-managed-settings-precedence] [^settings-reference-remotecontrolatstartup] [^settings-reference-isolatepeermachines] [^settings-reference-disableclaudeaiconnectors] [^settings-reference-crosssessioninbound] [^settings-reference-spinnertipsoverride] [^settings-reference-remote-defaultenvironmentid] [^settings-reference-forceloginmethod] [^settings-reference-forceloginorguuid] [^settings-reference-basheditdiffenabled] |
| `settings-project-autocontinue-off` | `autoContinueAtUsageLimit` in a project or local file turns the feature off for everyone whose user and managed settings leave it unset; it is not ignored. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^settings-reference-autocontinueatusagelimit] |
| `settings-removed-key` | Keys with no effect: `taskOutputMaxChars`, `keybindingFlavor`, `permissionExplainerEnabled`, `teammateDefaultModel`, `disableArtifact: false`; `includeCoAuthoredBy` once `attribution.commit` or `.pr` is set; `voiceEnabled` once `voice.enabled` is set. Appendix A.2. | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^settings-reference-taskoutputmaxchars] [^settings-reference-keybindingflavor] [^settings-reference-permissionexplainerenabled] [^settings-reference-teammatedefaultmodel] [^settings-reference-disableartifact] [^settings-reference-includecoauthoredby] [^settings-reference-voiceenabled] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `settings-deprecated-key` | Deprecated keys that still work: `includeCoAuthoredBy` (use `attribution`), `voiceEnabled` (use `voice.enabled`), `disableArtifact: true` (use `enableArtifact: false`), `ignorePatterns` (use `permissions.deny` `Read` rules). Appendix A.2. | recommended | warn | deprecated | – | [^settings-reference-includecoauthoredby] [^settings-reference-voiceenabled] [^settings-reference-disableartifact] [^settings-reference-permissions-deny] |
| `settings-attribution-false` | `attribution: false` needs v2.1.281 or later; earlier versions skip the whole file. In shared files use `{"commit": "", "pr": "", "sessionUrl": false}`. Fails only on older clients. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^settings-reference-attribution] |
| `settings-conflicting-keys` | Same-file pairs where one key voids another: `verbose`+`viewMode`; `spinnerTipsOverride`+`spinnerTipsEnabled: false`; `enableWorkflows: true`+`disableWorkflows: true`; `statusLine`/`subagentStatusLine`/`fileSuggestion`+`disableAllHooks: true`; `viewMode: "focus"` without `tui: "fullscreen"`; `vimInsertModeRemaps` without `editorMode: "vim"`; `disableAutoMode`+`defaultMode: "auto"`; `timeZone`+`timeFormat: "24-hour-utc"`; `allowedChannelPlugins` without `channelsEnabled: true`. | recommended | error | consistency | – | [^settings-reference-verbose] [^settings-reference-spinnertipsoverride] [^settings-reference-enableworkflows] [^settings-reference-status-line-and-file-suggestion-gates] [^settings-reference-viewmode] [^settings-reference-viminsertmoderemaps] [^settings-reference-disableautomode] [^settings-reference-timezone] [^channels-restrict-which-channel-plugins-can-run] |

| `keybindings-duplicate` | Same keystroke twice in one context, including case variants (`K` equals `k`; write `shift+k`). | recommended | error | consistency | – | [^keybindings-validation] [^keybindings-uppercase-letters] |
| `keybindings-chord-prefix` | Single key bound while a chord starting with it stays bound in any active context; unbind each such chord (`null`) in its own context. | recommended | error | consistency | – | [^keybindings-unbind-default-shortcuts] |
| `keybindings-removed-action` | Actions with no effect: `footer:dismiss` (v2.1.281), `confirm:toggleExplanation` (v2.1.257), `modelPicker:setAsDefault` (use `modelPicker:thisSessionOnly`), `doctor:fix` and the `Doctor` context (v2.1.205). | recommended | error | no-op | – | [^keybindings-footer-actions] [^keybindings-confirmation-actions] [^keybindings-contexts] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `keybindings-deprecated-action` | Legacy actions that still work: `messageSelector:*` (use `select:*`), `diff:viewDetails` (use `select:accept`). | recommended | warn | deprecated | – | [^keybindings-message-selector-actions] [^keybindings-diff-actions] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `keybindings-portability` | `cmd` or `super` modifiers, which most terminals never send; `ctrl+b` (tmux), `ctrl+a` (screen), `ctrl+z` (SIGTSTP) terminal conflicts. | recommended | warn | portability | – | [^keybindings-modifiers] [^keybindings-terminal-conflicts] [^changelog-claude-code-changelog] |
| `keybindings-legacy-confirm-yn` | `Confirmation` block binds `y` to `confirm:yes` and `n` to `confirm:no`, leftovers of a pre-v2.1.280 generated file that act on every dialog. | recommended | warn | practice | – | [^keybindings-confirmation-actions] |
| `keybindings-action-context` | (heuristic) Action belongs to its block's context (`chat:*` in `Chat`; `select:*` also in `MessageSelector`, `DiffDialog`, `Settings`). | off | warn | consistency | – | [^keybindings-available-actions] |

#### Appendix A: catalogs

##### A.1 Key scope (`settings-key-scope`, `settings-schema`)

Source: the Scope column of the settings index [^settings-reference-settings-index]. Nested keys are dotted. A key in no list, other than `$schema`, is unknown to `settings-schema`.

- **Managed only** (flag in every non-managed file), 43: allowAllClaudeAiMcps, allowManagedHooksOnly, allowManagedMcpServersOnly, allowManagedPermissionRulesOnly, allowedChannelPlugins, availableModelsMatch, blockedMarketplaces, browserExternalPageTools, channelsEnabled, claudeMd, deniedModels, disableBrowserExternalNavigation, disableCommandPluginSources, disableDesktopLocalSessions, disableMobileSimulatorTools, disableSideloadFlags, forceLoginGatewayUrl, forceRemoteSettingsRefresh, gatewayInternalNetworks, managedMcpServers, managedSourcesBehavior, modelPricing, parentSettingsBehavior, pluginSuggestionMarketplaces, pluginTrustMessage, policyHelper (.path, .refreshIntervalMs, .timeoutMs), requiredMaximumVersion, requiredMinimumVersion, sandbox.bwrapPath, sandbox.filesystem.allowManagedReadPathsOnly, sandbox.network.allowManagedDomainsOnly, sandbox.socatPath, sshHostAllowlist, strictKnownMarketplaces, strictPluginOnlyCustomization (.agents, .hooks, .mcp, .skills), wslInheritsWindowsSettings.

##### A.2 Deprecated and removed keys (`settings-deprecated-key`, `settings-removed-key`)

- **Deprecated, still work (warn)**: includeCoAuthoredBy (since v2.0.62, use attribution), voiceEnabled (since v2.1.92, use voice.enabled), disableArtifact: true (use enableArtifact: false), ignorePatterns (use permissions.deny Read rules).
- **Removed or no effect (error)**: taskOutputMaxChars (v2.1.277), keybindingFlavor (v2.1.261), permissionExplainerEnabled (v2.1.257), teammateDefaultModel (v2.1.234), disableArtifact: false, includeCoAuthoredBy with attribution.commit or attribution.pr, voiceEnabled with voice.enabled.

##### A.3 Env vars settings cannot set (`settings-env-ignored-var`, `settings-env-deprecated-var`)

- **Ignored from every settings file**: CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE, CLAUDE_CODE_ACCOUNT_UUID (and other hosting identity vars), CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET, CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN, CLAUDE_CODE_PROJECT_DIR_NAME, CLAUDE_CODE_RESTRICTED, CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_POWERSHELL_CMD_RM_DENY, CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_DANGEROUS_RM_TIMEOUT, CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_SUBSTITUTION_RM_PROMPT, CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ADMIN_ENV_UNION.
- **Set by Claude Code at runtime**: CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_SESSION, CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID, CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_SESSION_ID, CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_SESSION_ID, CLAUDE_EFFORT, CLAUDE_JOB_DIR, CLAUDE_CODE_PROVIDER_MANAGED_BY_HOST.
- **Ignored in project and local files** (set in shell, user, or managed settings): CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR, CLAUDE_CODE_TMPDIR, HOME, TMPDIR, TMP, TEMP, XDG_*; OTEL_LOG_RAW_API_BODIES, ENABLE_BETA_TRACING_DETAILED, BETA_TRACING_ENDPOINT; CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TELEMETRY, CLAUDE_CODE_ENHANCED_TELEMETRY_BETA, ENABLE_ENHANCED_TELEMETRY_BETA; OTEL_LOGS_EXPORTER, OTEL_METRICS_EXPORTER, OTEL_TRACES_EXPORTER (value `none` allowed); OTEL_LOG_USER_PROMPTS, OTEL_LOG_TOOL_CONTENT, OTEL_LOG_TOOL_DETAILS (off value such as `0` allowed), OTEL_LOG_ASSISTANT_RESPONSES, OTEL_LOG_MANAGED_SETTINGS; OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_* ending _ENDPOINT, _HEADERS, _PROTOCOL, _CERTIFICATE, _CLIENT_KEY, _INSECURE; OTEL_EXPORTER_PROMETHEUS_HOST, OTEL_EXPORTER_PROMETHEUS_PORT; CLAUDE_CODE_PROCESS_WRAPPER, CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_SKILLS, CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_PLUGINS, CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_CACHE_DIR, CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_SEED_DIR.
- **Do not flag in project files**: OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES and the export interval, timeout, and compression variables (e.g. OTEL_METRIC_EXPORT_INTERVAL).
- **Removed, no-op (error)**: CLAUDE_CODE_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (v2.1.186), CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_OPUS_4_7_FAST_MODE (v2.1.142), CLAUDE_CODE_OPUS_4_6_FAST_MODE_OVERRIDE (v2.1.160), CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENTS_PER_SESSION (v2.1.224), CLAUDE_SUBAGENT_BG_SHELL_MAX_MS (v2.1.260), TASK_MAX_OUTPUT_LENGTH (v2.1.277), CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_AUTO_MODE (no effect since v2.1.207).
- **Deprecated, still work (warn, `settings-env-deprecated-var`)**: ANTHROPIC_SMALL_FAST_MODEL (use ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_HAIKU_MODEL), ENABLE_PROMPT_CACHING_1H_BEDROCK (use ENABLE_PROMPT_CACHING_1H), DISABLE_BUG_COMMAND (use DISABLE_FEEDBACK_COMMAND), SLASH_COMMAND_TOOL_CHAR_BUDGET (legacy name), CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TASKS=0 (legacy TodoWrite tool).

##### A.4 Env value forms (`settings-env-value-format`)

- CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENT_SPAWN_DEPTH, CLAUDE_CODE_WEBFETCH_CACHE_TTL_MS: plain digits only. CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_CONCURRENT_SUBAGENTS: positive whole number.
- CLAUDE_CODE_FORK_SUBAGENT, CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_AGENT_TEAMS: `0` or `1`.
- CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW: plain integer 100000 to 1000000 (no `k` or `M`). BASH_MAX_OUTPUT_LENGTH: at most 150000.
- CLAUDE_CODE_TOOL_MEMORY_LIMIT: digits with optional K, M, G, T, or `0`, `off`, `false`, `no`, `none`. CLAUDE_CODE_TOOL_MEMORY_CGROUP_EXCLUDE: comma list of mcp, lsp, hooks, plugin, helper, agent, or `none`, `all-new`.
- ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH: `true`, `false`, `auto`, `auto:N` (N 0 to 100). MCP_SDK_GENERATION: `v1`, `v2`. MCP_PROTOCOL_NEGOTIATION: `auto`, `legacy`.
- CLAUDE_CODE_PROMPT_CACHE_TTL, CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_PROMPT_CACHE_TTL: `5m`, `1h`. CLAUDE_CODE_EFFORT_LEVEL: a level or `auto`, never `ultracode`.
- CLAUDE_CODE_SHELL: path to a `bash` or `zsh` binary. ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_*_SUPPORTED_CAPABILITIES: comma list of effort, xhigh_effort, max_effort, thinking, adaptive_thinking, interleaved_thinking.

##### A.5 Value constraints (`settings-schema`, keys this group owns)

- Booleans: alwaysThinkingEnabled, fastMode, fastModePerSessionOptIn, enforceAvailableModels, showThinkingSummaries, switchModelsOnFlag, ultracode, and every other key typed Boolean. Managed browserExternalPageTools, disableBrowserExternalNavigation, disableMobileSimulatorTools, disableDesktopLocalSessions take JSON `true` only.
- String `"disable"` only: disableAutoMode, disableDeepLinkRegistration.
- Model: advisorModel (see `settings-model-value`); availableModels, fallbackModel string arrays; availableModelsMatch `prefix`, `exact`; effortLevel `low`, `medium`, `high`, `xhigh`; maxEffortLevel adds `max`; modelOverrides object of strings; modelSettings maps model to `{effortLevel, maxEffortLevel}`; modelPicker.options rows need `model` (optional label, description, behavesAs), replaceBuiltInOptions Boolean; modelPricing.multiplier above 0 and at most 10, override rows need input, output, cacheRead, cacheWrite each 0 to 10000; promptCacheTtl, subagentPromptCacheTtl `5m`, `1h`.
- Limits: autoCompactWindow 100000 to 1000000; bashOutputMaxChars positive integer, clamped to 4000 to 128000; skillListingBudgetFraction above 0 and at most 1; skillListingMaxDescChars positive integer; maxProseWidth whole number of at least 40; cleanupPeriodDays whole number of at least 1; desktopSessionCleanupPeriodDays at least 0; feedbackSurveyRate 0 to 1.
- Enums: askUserQuestionTimeout, dialogExpiry `60s`, `5m`, `10m`, `never`; defaultShell `bash`, `powershell`; editorMode `normal`, `vim`; tui `default`, `fullscreen`; viewMode `default`, `verbose`, `focus`; teammateMode `in-process`, `auto`, `tmux`, `iterm2`; workflowSizeGuideline `unrestricted`, `small`, `medium`, `large`; skillOverrides values `on`, `name-only`, `user-invocable-only`, `off`; crossSessionInbound `accept`, `hold`, `refuse` (unknown value holds every inbound message); autoUpdatesChannel `latest`, `stable`; feedbackDrafts `notify`, `quiet`, `off`; forceLoginMethod `claudeai`, `console`, `gateway`; preferredNotifChannel `auto`, `terminal_bell`, `iterm2`, `iterm2_with_bell`, `kitty`, `ghostty`, `notifications_disabled`; managedSourcesBehavior, parentSettingsBehavior `first-wins`, `merge`; worktree.baseRef `fresh`, `head`; worktree.bgIsolation `worktree`, `none`.
- theme: `auto`, `dark`, `light`, `dark-daltonized`, `light-daltonized`, `dark-ansi`, `light-ansi`, `custom:<slug>`, `custom:<plugin>:<slug>`. timeFormat: `auto`, `12-hour`, `24-hour`, `24-hour-utc`, or a pattern with `%`. timeZone: IANA name.
- Shapes: attribution `{commit, pr, sessionUrl}` or `false`; statusLine `{type: "command", command, padding (integer, at least 0), refreshInterval (at least 1), hideVimModeIndicator}`; subagentStatusLine and fileSuggestion `{type: "command", command}`; voice `{enabled, autoSubmit, mode: hold or tap}`; spinnerVerbs `{mode: append or replace, verbs}`; companyAnnouncements string array; worktree keys baseRef, symlinkDirectories, sparsePaths, bgIsolation only; sshConfigs rows need id, name, sshHost; allowedChannelPlugins rows `{marketplace, plugin}`.
- spinnerTipsOverride: tips entries are strings or `{id, text, cooldownSessions, priority}`; id required, at most 64 of `[A-Za-z0-9._-]`, unique (first wins); text required, one line, at most 500 chars; cooldownSessions 0 to 1000; priority -10 to 10; at most 200 tips; tipsFile absolute or `~/` path; label at most 40 chars.
- footerLinksRegexes rows `{type: "regex", pattern, url, label}`; url scheme https, http, vscode, vscode-insiders, cursor, windsurf, zed, jetbrains, idea, slack, linear, notion, figma; `{name}` placeholders match named groups in `pattern`. spellcheck.checker `aspell`, `hunspell`, `ispell`, `auto`. vimInsertModeRemaps keys exactly two printable chars, value `"<Esc>"`.
- Paths and formats: plansDirectory relative and inside the project root; remote.defaultEnvironmentId starts `env_` or `ccpool_`; forceLoginOrgUUID UUID or UUID array; minimumVersion `N.N.N`; prUrlTemplate placeholders only `{host}`, `{owner}`, `{repo}`, `{number}`, `{url}`; policyHelper.path absolute and normalized (Windows `.exe`), policyHelper.timeoutMs at least 1000, policyHelper.refreshIntervalMs `0` or at least 60000.

- Contexts: Global, Chat, Autocomplete, Settings, Confirmation, Tabs, Help, Transcript, HistorySearch, Task, ThemePicker, Attachments, Footer, MessageSelector, DiffDialog, DiffPanel, ModelPicker, EffortSlider, Select, Plugin, Agents, Scroll.

- Actions: agents:switchView, agents:togglePin, app:cycleDiffBase, app:diffFileListDown, app:diffFileListUp, app:exit, app:interrupt, app:redraw, app:toggleDiffNoiseFilter, app:toggleDiffPreSession,
